    """Get current date."""

    """Validate system."""
    global WS_ERROR_FLAG
    if WS_CUST_STATUS != '00':

    if WS_ACCT_STATUS != '00':

        WS_ERROR_FLAG = 'Y'

def process_deposits_C1() -> None:
    """Process deposits."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    print("PROCESSING DEPOSITS...")
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
        validate_deposit_C1()
        if WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':
            post_deposit_C1()
            update_balance_C1()
            global WS_TRAN_COUNT
            WS_TRAN_COUNT += 1
        WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

def validate_deposit_C1() -> None:
    """Validate deposit."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG
    WS_VALID_FLAG = 'Y'
    if WS_CALC_AMOUNT < 0:
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
    if ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_status != 'A':
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'

    """Post deposit."""

def process_withdrawals_C1() -> None:

def validate_withdrawal_C1() -> None:
    """Validate withdrawal."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG
    WS_VALID_FLAG = 'Y'
    if WS_CALC_AMOUNT > ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_available:
        if WS_CALC_AMOUNT > (ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_available + ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_overdraft_limit):
            WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        else:
            apply_overdraft_fee_C1()

def apply_overdraft_fee_C1() -> None:
    """Apply overdraft fee."""
    global WS_OVERDRAFT_FEE, WS_TOTAL_FEES, ACCOUNT_RECORD
    WS_TOTAL_FEES += None  # TODO: was WS_OVERDRAFT_FEE
    ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_balance -= None  # TODO: was WS_OVERDRAFT_FEE

def post_withdrawal_C1() -> None:
    """Post withdrawal."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, ACCOUNT_RECORD, WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS
    ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_balance -= None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_available -= None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    write_transaction_C1()

def determine_rate_C1() -> None:
    """Determine rate."""
    global WS_CALC_RATE, WS_CHECKING_RATE, WS_SAVINGS_RATE, WS_MM_RATE, WS_CD_RATE_1YR
    if ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_type == "CH":
        WS_CALC_RATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_CHECKING_RATE
    elif ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_type == "SV":
        WS_CALC_RATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_SAVINGS_RATE
    elif ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_type == "MM":
        WS_CALC_RATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_MM_RATE
    elif ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_type == "CD":
        WS_CALC_RATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_CD_RATE_1YR
    else:
        WS_CALC_RATE = Decimal(0)

    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CALC_INTEREST, ACCOUNT_RECORD, WS_CALC_RATE
    WS_CALC_INTEREST = ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_balance * WS_CALC_RATE / 12

def post_interest_C1() -> None:
    """Post interest."""
    global WS_CALC_INTEREST, ACCOUNT_RECORD, WS_TOTAL_INTEREST
    ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_balance += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_INTEREST
    WS_TOTAL_INTEREST += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_INTEREST

def apply_fees_C1() -> None:
    """Apply monthly fees."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    print("APPLYING MONTHLY FEES...")
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
        check_minimum_balance_C1()
        if WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':
            waive_fee_C1()
        else:
            charge_fee_C1()
        WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

def check_minimum_balance_C1() -> None:
    """Check minimum balance."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG
    if ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_balance >= ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_min_balance:
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'

def waive_fee_C1() -> None:
    """Waive fee."""
    pass

def charge_fee_C1() -> None:
    """Charge fee."""
    global ACCOUNT_RECORD, WS_TOTAL_FEES
    ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_balance -= ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_monthly_fee
    WS_TOTAL_FEES += ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_monthly_fee

def process_payments_C1() -> None:
    """Process loan payments."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    print("PROCESSING LOAN PAYMENTS...")
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
        if LOAN_RECORD.loan_status == 'C':
            calculate_payment_C1()
            apply_payment_C1()
            update_loan_C1()
        WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

def calculate_payment_C1() -> None:
    """Calculate payment."""
    global WS_CALC_PAYMENT, LOAN_RECORD, WS_CALC_INTEREST, WS_CALC_PRINCIPAL
    WS_CALC_PAYMENT = LOAN_RECORD.loan_payment_amount
    WS_CALC_INTEREST = LOAN_RECORD.loan_current_balance * LOAN_RECORD.loan_interest_rate / 12
    WS_CALC_PRINCIPAL = WS_CALC_PAYMENT - WS_CALC_INTEREST

def apply_payment_C1() -> None:
    """Apply payment."""
    global WS_CALC_PRINCIPAL, LOAN_RECORD, WS_TOTAL_PAYMENTS, WS_CALC_PAYMENT, WS_TOTAL_INTEREST, WS_CALC_INTEREST
    LOAN_RECORD.loan_current_balance -= None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_PRINCIPAL
    WS_TOTAL_PAYMENTS += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_PAYMENT
    WS_TOTAL_INTEREST += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_INTEREST

def update_loan_C1() -> None:
    """Update loan."""
    global LOAN_RECORD
    if LOAN_RECORD.loan_current_balance <= 0:
        LOAN_RECORD.loan_status = 'P'

def assess_delinquencies_C1() -> None:
    """Assess delinquent loans."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    print("ASSESSING DELINQUENT LOANS...")
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
        check_payment_status_C1()
        if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':
            mark_delinquent_C1()
            assess_late_fee_C1()
        WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

def check_payment_status_C1() -> None:
    """Check payment status."""
    global WS_FOUND_FLAG, LOAN_RECORD, WS_CURRENT_DATE
    if LOAN_RECORD.loan_next_payment_date < WS_CURRENT_DATE:
        WS_FOUND_FLAG = 'N'

    LOAN_RECORD.loan_status = 'D'

def anonymous_4210_determine_base_premium_C2() -> None:
    """Determine base premium."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    if INS_LIFE:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / 1000 * WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000
    elif INS_HEALTH:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM
    elif INS_AUTO:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM
    elif INS_HOME:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / 1000 * WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000
    elif INS_UMBRELLA:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_UMBRELLA_RATE

def anonymous_4220_apply_risk_factor_C2() -> None:
    """Apply risk factor."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    if INS_CLAIMS_COUNT > 2:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal('1.25')

def anonymous_8500_calculate_tax_C2() -> None:
    """Calculate tax."""
    global WS_CALC_TAX
    if WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_1_MAX:
        WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE
    elif WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_2_MAX:
        WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + \
                      ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE)
    elif WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_3_MAX:
        WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + \
                      ((WS_BRACKET_2_MAX - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) + \
                      ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_2_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_3_RATE)
    else:
        WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_5_RATE

def anonymous_7410_calculate_risk_score_C2() -> None:
    """Calculate risk score."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    WS_CALC_RESULT = Decimal('0.00')
    if CUST_CREDIT_SCORE < 600:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 30
    if CUST_TOTAL_LOANS > CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 20

    """Update customer profile."""

        CUST_RISK_RATING = 'H'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 25:
        CUST_RISK_RATING = 'M'
    else:
        CUST_RISK_RATING = 'L'

def anonymous_7821_dti_calculation_C2() -> None:
    """DTI calculation."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, WS_NOT_APPROVED
    WS_CALC_RESULT = LOAN_PAYMENT_AMOUNT / (CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE / 12)
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > Decimal('0.43'):
        WS_NOT_APPROVED = True

def anonymous_7822_ltv_calculation_C2() -> None:
    """LTV calculation."""
    global LOAN_LTV_RATIO, WS_CALC_FEE
    LOAN_LTV_RATIO = LOAN_CURRENT_BALANCE / LOAN_COLLATERAL_VALUE
    if LOAN_LTV_RATIO > Decimal('0.80'):
        WS_CALC_FEE += WS_LOAN_ORIGINATION_PCT

def anonymous_7823_credit_analysis_C2() -> None:
    """Credit analysis."""

def customer_segmentation_C3() -> None:
    """Segments customers."""
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF, CUSTOMER_MASTER
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Simulate READ customer_master NEXT with a list of customer data
        if CUSTOMER_MASTER is None:
            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            calculate_clv_C3()
            assign_segment_C3()
            WS_EOF = True

def calculate_clv_C3() -> None:
    """Calculates customer lifetime value."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, CUSTOMER_MASTER
    if CUSTOMER_MASTER:
        WS_CALC_RESULT = (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_balance * WS_SAVINGS_RATE) + (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_loans * WS_PERSONAL_RATE) + (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_investments * Decimal('0.01'))

def assign_segment_C3() -> None:
    """Assigns customer segment."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, WS_TEMP_CODE
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 10000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'PLATINUM'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 5000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'GOLD'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 1000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'SILVER'
    else:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'BRONZE'

def default_prediction_C3() -> None:
    """Predicts defaults."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, LOAN, CUSTOMER_MASTER
    if LOAN and LOAN.loan_delinquent:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 25
    if CUSTOMER_MASTER and CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_credit_score < 600:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 30

def sweep_accounts_C3() -> None:
    """Handles sweep accounts."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, ACCT_BALANCE, ACCT_MIN_BALANCE, WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS
    if ACCT_BALANCE > ACCT_MIN_BALANCE:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ACCT_BALANCE - ACCT_MIN_BALANCE
        ACCT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT
        WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def extract_data_A110_C3() -> None:
    """Extracts data."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF, WS_PROCESS_COUNT, CUSTOMER_MASTER
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Simulate READ customer_master NEXT with a list of customer data
        if CUSTOMER_MASTER is None:
            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1
            WS_EOF = True

def cleanse_data_A121_C3() -> None:
    """Cleanses data."""
    global CUSTOMER_MASTER
    if CUSTOMER_MASTER and not CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_name:
        CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_last_name = "UNKNOWN"

def standardize_data_A122_C3() -> None:
    """Standardizes data."""
    global CUSTOMER_MASTER
    if CUSTOMER_MASTER:
        CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_state = CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_state.upper()

    """Checks completeness."""
    global WS_ERROR_COUNT, CUSTOMER_MASTER

def accuracy_check_A220_C3() -> None:
    """Checks accuracy."""
    global WS_ERROR_COUNT, CUSTOMER_MASTER
    if CUSTOMER_MASTER and (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_credit_score < 300 or CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_credit_score > 850):
        WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1

def consistency_check_A230_C3() -> None:
    """Checks consistency."""

def timeliness_check_A240_C3() -> None:
    """Checks timeliness."""
    global WS_CURRENT_DATE, CUSTOMER_MASTER, WS_ERROR_COUNT
    if CUSTOMER_MASTER and CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_last_activity < WS_CURRENT_DATE - 365:
        pass

@dataclass
class DataStructureC4:
    """Data structure."""
    cust_ssn: Optional[str] = None
    cust_credit_score: Optional[int] = None
    cust_total_balance: Optional[int] = None
    cust_total_loans: Optional[int] = None
    cust_risk_rating: Optional[str] = None
    loan_current_balance: Optional[int] = None
    cust_status: Optional[str] = None
    loan_paid_off: Optional[bool] = False
    tran_amount: Optional[int] = None

ws_temp_code: str = ""
ws_calc_result: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_calc_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_total_deposits: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_total_fees: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_atm_fee_foreign: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_process_count: int = 0
ws_error_count: int = 0
ws_not_eof: bool = False
ws_eof: bool = False
transaction_log: str = ""
ws_current_timestamp: str = ""
ws_temp_string: str = ""
ws_valid: bool = False
ws_formatted_count: str = ""
ws_cust_count: int = 0
data_structure_C4 = DataStructureC4()

def c100_transaction_monitoring_C4() -> None:
    """Monitoring transactions."""
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")
    global ws_not_eof
    ws_not_eof = True
    while not ws_eof:
        # READ transaction_log NEXT
        # Assume read sets ws_eof to True at end of file
        if ws_eof:
            break
        c110_rule_based_detection_C4()
        c120_behavior_analysis_C4()
        c130_network_analysis_C4()

def c110_rule_based_detection_C4() -> None:
    """Rule-based detection."""
    if data_structure_C4.tran_amount is not None and data_structure_C4.tran_amount >= 10000:
        c111_flag_ctr_C4()
    if data_structure_C4.tran_amount is not None and 5000 <= data_structure_C4.tran_amount < 10000:
        c112_check_structuring_C4()

def c111_flag_ctr_C4() -> None:
    """Flag CTR."""
    global ws_process_count
    ws_process_count += 1

def c112_check_structuring_C4() -> None:
    """Check structuring."""
    global ws_error_count
    ws_error_count += 1

def d110_classification_C4() -> None:
    """Classification."""
    if data_structure_C4.cust_credit_score is not None and data_structure_C4.cust_credit_score > 750:
        data_structure_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'A'
    elif data_structure_C4.cust_credit_score is not None and data_structure_C4.cust_credit_score > 650:
        data_structure_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'B'
    elif data_structure_C4.cust_credit_score is not None and data_structure_C4.cust_credit_score > 550:
        data_structure_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'C'
    else:
        data_structure_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'D'

def d120_regression_C4() -> None:
    """Regression."""
    global ws_calc_result
    if data_structure_C4.cust_credit_score is not None and data_structure_C4.cust_total_balance is not None and data_structure_C4.cust_total_loans is not None:
        ws_calc_result = Decimal((data_structure_C4.cust_credit_score * 10) + 0  # TODO
                           (data_structure_C4.cust_total_balance / 1000) - 0  # TODO
                           (data_structure_C4.cust_total_loans / 2000))

    """Clustering."""

def main_loop_C5() -> None:
    """Main processing loop."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        try:
            customer_record  = None  # TODO: was CUSTOMER_MASTER
        except EOFError:
            WS_EOF = True
            continue
        i110_update_profile_C5()
        i120_enrich_profile_C5()
        global WS_CUST_COUNT
        WS_CUST_COUNT += 1

def main_control_0000_C5() -> None:

def initialization_1000_C5() -> None:
    """Initialize work areas, counters, and open files."""
    global WS_WORK_AREAS, WS_COUNTERS, WS_TOTALS, WS_CURRENT_DATETIME, WS_CURR_YEAR, WS_CURR_MONTH, WS_CURR_DAY, RPT_YEAR, RPT_MONTH, RPT_DAY
    WS_WORK_AREAS = None
    WS_COUNTERS = None
    WS_TOTALS = None
    WS_CURRENT_DATETIME = "current date"
    WS_CURR_YEAR = "year"
    WS_CURR_MONTH = "month"
    WS_CURR_DAY = "day"
    RPT_YEAR  = None  # TODO: was WS_CURR_YEAR
    RPT_MONTH  = None  # TODO: was WS_CURR_MONTH
    RPT_DAY  = None  # TODO: was WS_CURR_DAY
    open_files_1100_C5()
    read_parameters_1200_C5()
    initialize_tables_1300_C5()
    load_reference_data_1400_C5()

def open_files_1100_C5() -> None:
    """Open input and output files."""
    global WS_FILE_STATUS, WS_ERROR_MSG
    WS_FILE_STATUS = '00'
    if WS_FILE_STATUS != '00':
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'FILE OPEN ERROR'
        abort_process_9500_C5()

def read_parameters_1200_C5() -> None:
    """Accept system parameters."""
    global WS_PARAM_DATE, WS_PARAM_TIME, WS_JOB_ID, WS_ENV_TYPE, WS_PROCESS_DATE
    WS_PARAM_DATE = "today"
    WS_PARAM_TIME = "now"
    WS_JOB_ID = 'batch_001'
    WS_ENV_TYPE = 'PRODUCTION'
    WS_PROCESS_DATE = 0

def initialize_tables_1300_C5() -> None:
    """Initialize rate and branch tables."""
    global WS_TBL_IDX, RATE_TABLE_ENTRY, BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY, RT_RATE, RT_CODE
    WS_TBL_IDX = 1
    while WS_TBL_IDX <= 100:
        RATE_TABLE_ENTRY.append("table entry")
        RT_RATE.append(Decimal("0.00"))
        RT_CODE.append("")
        WS_TBL_IDX += 1
    WS_TBL_IDX = 1
    while WS_TBL_IDX <= 50:
        BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY.append("branch")
        WS_TBL_IDX += 1

def load_reference_data_1400_C5() -> None:
    """Load reference data from file into table."""
    global WS_TBL_IDX, WS_EOF_FLAG, REFERENCE_FILE, WS_REF_RECORD, WS_REF_CODE, WS_REF_RATE, RT_CODE, RT_RATE
    WS_TBL_IDX = 1
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y' and WS_TBL_IDX <= 100:
        try:
            WS_REF_RECORD  = None  # TODO: was REFERENCE_FILE
        except EOFError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
            break
        WS_REF_CODE = "code"
        WS_REF_RATE = Decimal("1.00")
        RT_CODE.append(WS_REF_CODE)
        RT_RATE.append(WS_REF_RATE)
        WS_TBL_IDX += 1
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def process_transactions_2000_C5() -> None:
    """Process transaction records from file."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, TRANSACTION_FILE, WS_TRANSACTION_REC, WS_TRANS_COUNT
    try:
        WS_TRANSACTION_REC  = None  # TODO: was TRANSACTION_FILE
    except EOFError:
        WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        return
    WS_TRANS_COUNT += 1
    validate_transaction_2100_C5()
    if WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':
        process_by_type_2200_C5()
    else:
        handle_error_2900_C5()

def validate_transaction_2100_C5() -> None:
    """Validate transaction record."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG, WS_ERROR_MSG, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, TXN_AMOUNT, TXN_TYPE
    WS_VALID_FLAG = 'Y'
    if TXN_ACCOUNT_ID == "" or TXN_ACCOUNT_ID is None:
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'
        return
    if not str(TXN_AMOUNT).replace('.', '', 1).isdigit():
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
        return
    if TXN_TYPE not in ('D', 'W', 'T', 'I'):
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE'
    validate_account_exists_2150_C5()
    validate_business_rules_2160_C5()

def validate_account_exists_2150_C5() -> None:
    """Validate if account exists in master file."""
    global WS_SEARCH_KEY, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was TXN_ACCOUNT_ID
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'

def validate_business_rules_2160_C5() -> None:
    """Validate business rules based on transaction type."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG, WS_ERROR_MSG, TXN_TYPE, TXN_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    if TXN_TYPE == 'W':
        if TXN_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
            WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
            WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if TXN_AMOUNT > 1000000:
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT'

def process_by_type_2200_C5() -> None:
    """Process transaction based on transaction type."""
    global TXN_TYPE
    if TXN_TYPE == 'D':
        process_deposit_2300_C5()
    elif TXN_TYPE == 'W':
        process_withdrawal_2400_C5()
    elif TXN_TYPE == 'T':
        process_transfer_2500_C5()
    elif TXN_TYPE == 'I':
        process_interest_2600_C5()
    else:
        handle_error_2900_C5()

def process_deposit_2300_C5() -> None:
    """Process deposit transaction."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, TXN_AMOUNT, WS_TXN_DESC, WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS, WS_DEPOSIT_COUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was TXN_AMOUNT
    WS_TXN_DESC = 'DEPOSIT'
    WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS += None  # TODO: was TXN_AMOUNT
    WS_DEPOSIT_COUNT += 1
    update_account_2350_C5()
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()

def update_account_2350_C5() -> None:
    """Update account record in master file."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, ACCOUNT_RECORD, ACCT_BALANCE, WS_FILE_STATUS, WS_ERROR_MSG
    ACCT_BALANCE  = None  # TODO: was WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    ACCOUNT_RECORD = None
    WS_FILE_STATUS = '00'
    if WS_FILE_STATUS != '00':
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'UPDATE FAILED'
        handle_error_2900_C5()

def write_audit_trail_2380_C5() -> None:
    """Write audit trail record to audit file."""
    global WS_AUDIT_RECORD, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, TXN_AMOUNT, TXN_TYPE, WS_JOB_ID
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD = None
    AUDIT_ACCOUNT  = None  # TODO: was TXN_ACCOUNT_ID
    AUDIT_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was TXN_AMOUNT
    AUDIT_TYPE  = None  # TODO: was TXN_TYPE
    AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = "current date"
    AUDIT_JOB_ID  = None  # TODO: was WS_JOB_ID

def process_withdrawal_2400_C5() -> None:
    """Process withdrawal transaction."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, TXN_AMOUNT, WS_TXN_DESC, WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS, WS_WITHDRAWAL_COUNT, WS_MIN_BALANCE_LIMIT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was TXN_AMOUNT
    WS_TXN_DESC = 'WITHDRAWAL'
    WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS += None  # TODO: was TXN_AMOUNT
    WS_WITHDRAWAL_COUNT += 1
    update_account_2350_C5()
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()
    if WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE < WS_MIN_BALANCE_LIMIT:
        generate_low_balance_alert_2450_C5()

def generate_low_balance_alert_2450_C5() -> None:
    """Generate low balance alert record."""
    global WS_ALERT_RECORD, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_ALERT_COUNT
    WS_ALERT_RECORD = None
    ALERT_TYPE = 'low_bal'
    ALERT_ACCOUNT  = None  # TODO: was TXN_ACCOUNT_ID
    ALERT_BALANCE  = None  # TODO: was WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    ALERT_DATE = "current date"
    WS_ALERT_COUNT += 1

def process_transfer_2500_C5() -> None:
    """Process transfer transaction."""
    validate_target_account_2510_C5()
    if WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':
        debit_source_2520_C5()
        credit_target_2530_C5()
        record_transfer_2540_C5()
    else:
        handle_error_2900_C5()

def validate_target_account_2510_C5() -> None:
    """Validate target account for transfer."""
    global WS_SEARCH_KEY, TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'TARGET ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'

def debit_source_2520_C5() -> None:
    """Debit the source account in transfer."""
    global TXN_AMOUNT, WS_SOURCE_BALANCE, ACCOUNT_RECORD, ACCT_BALANCE
    WS_SOURCE_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was TXN_AMOUNT
    ACCT_BALANCE  = None  # TODO: was WS_SOURCE_BALANCE
    ACCOUNT_RECORD = None

def credit_target_2530_C5() -> None:
    """Credit the target account in transfer."""
    global TXN_AMOUNT, WS_TARGET_BALANCE, ACCT_ID, TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_REC, ACCOUNT_RECORD, ACCT_BALANCE
    WS_TARGET_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was TXN_AMOUNT
    ACCT_ID  = None  # TODO: was TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_REC = None
    ACCT_BALANCE  = None  # TODO: was WS_TARGET_BALANCE
    ACCOUNT_RECORD = None

def record_transfer_2540_C5() -> None:
    """Record transfer transaction."""
    global TXN_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_TRANSFERS, WS_TRANSFER_COUNT
    WS_TOTAL_TRANSFERS += None  # TODO: was TXN_AMOUNT
    WS_TRANSFER_COUNT += 1
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()

def process_interest_2600_C5() -> None:
    """Process interest calculation and posting."""
    global WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_INTEREST_RATE, WS_TXN_DESC, WS_TOTAL_INTEREST, WS_INTEREST_COUNT
    WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT = WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE * WS_INTEREST_RATE / 100
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT
    WS_TXN_DESC = 'INTEREST'
    WS_TOTAL_INTEREST += None  # TODO: was WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT
    WS_INTEREST_COUNT += 1
    update_account_2350_C5()
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()

def handle_error_2900_C5() -> None:
    """Handle transaction error."""
    global WS_ERROR_COUNT, WS_ERROR_RECORD, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, WS_ERROR_MSG, WS_MAX_ERRORS, WS_ABORT_REASON
    WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1
    WS_ERROR_RECORD = None
    ERR_ACCOUNT  = None  # TODO: was TXN_ACCOUNT_ID
    ERR_MESSAGE  = None  # TODO: was WS_ERROR_MSG
    ERR_TIMESTAMP = "current date"
    if WS_ERROR_COUNT > WS_MAX_ERRORS:
        WS_ABORT_REASON = 'MAX ERRORS EXCEEDED'
        abort_process_9500_C5()

def batch_processing_3000_C5() -> None:
    """Process batch records from batch file."""
    load_batch_header_3100_C5()
    while WS_BATCH_EOF != 'Y':
        process_batch_items_3200_C5()
    validate_batch_totals_3300_C5()
    commit_batch_3400_C5()

def load_batch_header_3100_C5() -> None:
    """Load batch header record from file."""
    global WS_BATCH_EOF, BATCH_FILE, WS_BATCH_HEADER, BATCH_ID, BATCH_COUNT, BATCH_TOTAL, WS_CURRENT_BATCH, WS_EXPECTED_COUNT, WS_EXPECTED_TOTAL
    try:
        WS_BATCH_HEADER  = None  # TODO: was BATCH_FILE
    except EOFError:
        WS_BATCH_EOF = 'Y'
        return
    WS_CURRENT_BATCH  = None  # TODO: was BATCH_ID
    WS_EXPECTED_COUNT  = None  # TODO: was BATCH_COUNT
    WS_EXPECTED_TOTAL  = None  # TODO: was BATCH_TOTAL

def process_batch_items_3200_C5() -> None:
    """Process batch item records from file."""
    global WS_BATCH_EOF, BATCH_FILE, WS_BATCH_ITEM, WS_ACTUAL_COUNT, WS_ACTUAL_TOTAL, ITEM_AMOUNT
    try:
        WS_BATCH_ITEM  = None  # TODO: was BATCH_FILE
    except EOFError:
        WS_BATCH_EOF = 'Y'
        return
    WS_ACTUAL_COUNT += 1
    WS_ACTUAL_TOTAL += None  # TODO: was ITEM_AMOUNT
    process_single_item_3250_C5()

def process_single_item_3250_C5() -> None:
    """Process a single batch item record."""
    global ITEM_TYPE
    if ITEM_TYPE == 'PAY':
        process_payment_3260_C5()
    elif ITEM_TYPE == 'REF':
        process_refund_3270_C5()
    elif ITEM_TYPE == 'ADJ':
        process_adjustment_3280_C5()

def process_payment_3260_C5() -> None:
    """Process payment batch item."""
    global ITEM_ACCOUNT, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ITEM_ACCOUNT
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        global ITEM_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_PAYMENT_COUNT
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was ITEM_AMOUNT
        update_account_2350_C5()
        WS_PAYMENT_COUNT += 1

def process_refund_3270_C5() -> None:
    """Process refund batch item."""
    global ITEM_ACCOUNT, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ITEM_ACCOUNT
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        global ITEM_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_REFUND_COUNT
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was ITEM_AMOUNT
        update_account_2350_C5()
        WS_REFUND_COUNT += 1

def process_adjustment_3280_C5() -> None:
    """Process adjustment batch item."""
    global ITEM_ACCOUNT, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ITEM_ACCOUNT
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        global ITEM_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_ADJUSTMENT_COUNT
        if ITEM_AMOUNT > 0:
            WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was ITEM_AMOUNT
        else:
            WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was ITEM_AMOUNT
        update_account_2350_C5()
        WS_ADJUSTMENT_COUNT += 1

def validate_batch_totals_3300_C5() -> None:
    """Validate actual batch totals against expected totals."""
    global WS_ACTUAL_COUNT, WS_EXPECTED_COUNT, WS_ACTUAL_TOTAL, WS_EXPECTED_TOTAL, WS_ERROR_MSG
    if WS_ACTUAL_COUNT != WS_EXPECTED_COUNT:
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'BATCH COUNT MISMATCH'
        reject_batch_3350_C5()
    if WS_ACTUAL_TOTAL != WS_EXPECTED_TOTAL:
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'BATCH TOTAL MISMATCH'
        reject_batch_3350_C5()

def reject_batch_3350_C5() -> None:
    """Reject batch due to invalid totals."""
    global WS_REJECTION_RECORD, WS_CURRENT_BATCH, WS_ERROR_MSG, WS_REJECTED_BATCH_COUNT
    WS_REJECTION_RECORD = None
    REJ_BATCH_ID  = None  # TODO: was WS_CURRENT_BATCH
    REJ_REASON  = None  # TODO: was WS_ERROR_MSG
    REJ_DATE = "current date"
    WS_REJECTED_BATCH_COUNT += 1

def commit_batch_3400_C5() -> None:
    """Commit batch if valid."""
    global WS_BATCH_VALID
    if WS_BATCH_VALID == 'Y':
        global WS_COMMITTED_BATCH_COUNT
        WS_COMMITTED_BATCH_COUNT += 1
        update_batch_status_3450_C5()

def update_batch_status_3450_C5() -> None:
    """Update batch status to committed."""
    global BATCH_STATUS, BATCH_COMMIT_DATE, BATCH_HEADER_RECORD
    BATCH_STATUS = 'COMMITTED'
    BATCH_COMMIT_DATE = "current date"
    BATCH_HEADER_RECORD = None

def reporting_4000_C5() -> None:

def set_interest_rate_C6(account_type: str) -> None:
    """Set the interest rate based on account type."""
    if account_type == 'CHK':
        WS_INTEREST_RATE_C6 = Decimal("1.5")
    elif account_type == 'SAV':
        WS_INTEREST_RATE_C6 = Decimal("2.0")
    else:
        WS_INTEREST_RATE_C6 = Decimal("2.5")

def calculate_simple_interest_C6() -> None:
    """Calculate simple interest."""
    global WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST_C6
    WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST_C6 = (
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 * WS_INTEREST_RATE_C6 * WS_DAYS_IN_PERIOD_C6 / 36500
    )

def calculate_compound_interest_C6() -> None:
    """Calculate compound interest."""
    global WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR_C6, WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST_C6
    WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR_C6 = (1 + WS_INTEREST_RATE_C6 / 36500) ** WS_DAYS_IN_PERIOD_C6
    WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST_C6 = WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 * (WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR_C6 - 1)

def apply_interest_C6() -> None:
    """Apply interest to the account balance."""
    if WS_INTEREST_METHOD_C6 == 'S':
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 += WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST_C6
    else:
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 += WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST_C6
    update_account_2350_C6()

def fee_processing_C6() -> None:
    """Process fees for an account."""
    calculate_monthly_fee_8100_C6()
    calculate_transaction_fees_8200_C6()
    apply_fee_waivers_8300_C6()
    deduct_fees_8400_C6()

def calculate_monthly_fee_8100_C6() -> None:
    """Calculate monthly fee based on account type."""
    global WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6
    if WS_ACCOUNT_TYPE_C6 == 'CHK':
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = Decimal("12.00")
    elif WS_ACCOUNT_TYPE_C6 == 'SAV':
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = Decimal("5.00")
    elif WS_ACCOUNT_TYPE_C6 == 'PRM':
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = Decimal("25.00")
    else:
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = Decimal("0.00")

def calculate_transaction_fees_8200_C6() -> None:
    """Calculate transaction fees if transaction count exceeds the limit."""
    global WS_TRANS_FEE_C6, WS_EXCESS_TRANS_C6
    if WS_TRANS_COUNT_C6 > WS_FREE_TRANS_LIMIT_C6:
        WS_EXCESS_TRANS_C6 = WS_TRANS_COUNT_C6 - WS_FREE_TRANS_LIMIT_C6
        WS_TRANS_FEE_C6 = WS_EXCESS_TRANS_C6 * WS_PER_TRANS_FEE_C6
    else:
        WS_TRANS_FEE_C6 = Decimal("0.00")

def apply_fee_waivers_8300_C6() -> None:
    """Apply fee waivers based on account balance and customer tier."""
    global WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6, WS_TRANS_FEE_C6
    if WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 >= WS_MIN_BALANCE_WAIVER_C6:
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = Decimal("0.00")
    if WS_CUSTOMER_TIER_C6 == 'GOLD' or WS_CUSTOMER_TIER_C6 == 'PLATINUM':
        WS_TRANS_FEE_C6 = WS_TRANS_FEE_C6 * Decimal("0.5")

def deduct_fees_8400_C6() -> None:
    """Deduct monthly and transaction fees from the account balance."""
    global WS_TOTAL_FEES_C6, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6
    WS_TOTAL_FEES_C6 = WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 + WS_TRANS_FEE_C6
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 -= None  # TODO: was WS_TOTAL_FEES_C6
    update_account_2350_C6()
    record_fee_transaction_8450_C6()

def record_fee_transaction_8450_C6() -> None:
    """Record the fee transaction details."""
    global FEE_ACCOUNT_C6, FEE_AMOUNT_C6, FEE_DESCRIPTION_C6, FEE_DATE_C6
    #INITIALIZE ws_fee_record
    FEE_ACCOUNT_C6  = None  # TODO: was TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C6
    FEE_AMOUNT_C6  = None  # TODO: was WS_TOTAL_FEES_C6
    FEE_DESCRIPTION_C6 = 'MONTHLY FEE'
    FEE_DATE_C6 = int(datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d"))
    #WRITE fee_record FROM ws_fee_record
    pass

def loan_processing_10000_C6(loan_processing_area: WsLoanProcessingAreaC6, credit_scoring_area: WsCreditScoringAreaC6, risk_assessment_area: WsRiskAssessmentAreaC6, mortgage_details: WsMortgageDetailsC6) -> None:
    """Processes a loan application."""
    validate_loan_application_10100_C6(loan_processing_area)
    if WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 == 'Y':
        calculate_credit_score_10200_C6(credit_scoring_area)
        assess_risk_10300_C6(loan_processing_area, credit_scoring_area, risk_assessment_area, mortgage_details)
        determine_approval_10400_C6()
        if risk_assessment_area.ws_approval_status == 'A':
            generate_loan_terms_10500_C6()
            create_amortization_10600_C6()
            finalize_loan_10700_C6()
        else:
            process_decline_10800_C6()

def validate_loan_application_10100_C6(loan_processing_area: WsLoanProcessingAreaC6) -> None:
    """Validates the loan application."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG_C6, WS_ERROR_MSG_C6
    WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 = 'Y'
    if loan_processing_area.ws_loan_amount < 1000:
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C6 = 'MINIMUM LOAN AMOUNT IS $1000'
        return
    if loan_processing_area.ws_loan_amount > 10000000:
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C6 = 'MAXIMUM LOAN AMOUNT EXCEEDED'
        return
    if loan_processing_area.ws_loan_term_months < 6 or loan_processing_area.ws_loan_term_months > 360:
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C6 = 'INVALID LOAN TERM'

def calculate_credit_score_10200_C6(credit_scoring_area: WsCreditScoringAreaC6) -> None:
    """Calculates the credit score."""
    credit_scoring_area.ws_credit_score = 0
    score_payment_history_10210_C6(credit_scoring_area.ws_payment_history, credit_scoring_area)
    score_credit_utilization_10220_C6(credit_scoring_area)
    score_credit_length_10230_C6(credit_scoring_area)
    score_new_credit_10240_C6(credit_scoring_area)
    score_credit_mix_10250_C6(credit_scoring_area)
    determine_tier_10260_C6(credit_scoring_area)

def score_payment_history_10210_C6(payment_history: PaymentHistoryC6, credit_scoring_area: WsCreditScoringAreaC6) -> None:
    """Scores the payment history."""
    global WS_PAYMENT_SCORE_C6
    total_payments = (payment_history.ws_on_time_payments + payment_history.ws_late_30_days + 0  # TODO
                      payment_history.ws_late_60_days + payment_history.ws_late_90_days)
    if total_payments == 0:
        WS_PAYMENT_SCORE_C6 = 0
    else:
        WS_PAYMENT_SCORE_C6 = (payment_history.ws_on_time_payments * 100) / total_payments
    WS_PAYMENT_SCORE_C6 = WS_PAYMENT_SCORE_C6 * 0.35
    credit_scoring_area.ws_credit_score += int(WS_PAYMENT_SCORE_C6)

def score_credit_utilization_10220_C6(credit_scoring_area: WsCreditScoringAreaC6) -> None:
    """Scores the credit utilization."""
    global WS_

def calculate_pmi_C7(ws_ltv_ratio: float, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Calculates PMI amount based on LTV ratio."""
    if ws_ltv_ratio > 95:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0125') / 12
    elif ws_ltv_ratio > 90:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0100') / 12
    elif ws_ltv_ratio > 85:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0075') / 12
    else:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0050') / 12
    return ws_pmi_amount

def evaluate_history_C7(ws_late_90_days: int, ws_late_60_days: int, ws_late_30_days: int, ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str, str, str]:
    """Evaluates loan history and adjusts risk score."""
    ws_factor_1 = ""
    ws_factor_2 = ""
    ws_factor_3 = ""
    if ws_late_90_days > 0:
        ws_risk_score -= 50
        ws_factor_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
    if ws_late_60_days > 2:
        ws_risk_score -= 30
        ws_factor_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
    if ws_late_30_days > 5:
        ws_risk_score -= 20
        ws_factor_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'
    return ws_risk_score, ws_factor_1, ws_factor_2, ws_factor_3

def calculate_final_risk_C7(ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str]:
    """Calculates final risk score and category."""
    ws_risk_score = ws_risk_score / 4
    ws_risk_category = ""
    if ws_risk_score >= 80:
        ws_risk_category = 'LOW RISK'
    elif ws_risk_score >= 60:
        ws_risk_category = 'MODERATE'
    elif ws_risk_score >= 40:
        ws_risk_category = 'ELEVATED'
    else:
        ws_risk_category = 'HIGH RISK'
    return ws_risk_score, ws_risk_category

def determine_approval_C7(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_risk_category: str, ws_dti_ratio: float, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_base_rate: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Determines loan approval status."""
    ws_approval_status = ""
    ws_conditions = ""
    ws_approved_amount = Decimal(0)
    ws_approved_rate = Decimal(0)
    if ws_credit_tier == 'F':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_risk_category == 'HIGH RISK':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_dti_ratio > 50:
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate
    ws_approval_status = 'A'
    ws_approved_amount = ws_loan_amount
    approved_terms = calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_credit_tier, ws_base_rate, ws_risk_category)
    ws_approved_rate = approved_terms
    return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate

def calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_base_rate: Decimal, ws_risk_category: str) -> Decimal:
    """Calculates approved loan terms."""
    ws_approved_rate = Decimal(0)
    if ws_credit_tier == 'A':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.00')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'B':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.50')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'C':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('1.50')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'D':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('3.00')
    if ws_risk_category == 'ELEVATED':
        ws_approved_rate += Decimal('0.50')
    return ws_approved_rate

def generate_loan_terms_C7(ws_approved_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Generates loan terms."""
    ws_loan_interest_rate = ws_approved_rate
    ws_monthly_rate = ws_loan_interest_rate / 1200
    ws_compound_factor = (1 + ws_monthly_rate) ** ws_loan_term_months
    ws_loan_monthly_pmt = ws_loan_amount * ws_monthly_rate * ws_compound_factor / (ws_compound_factor - 1)
    ws_loan_principal_bal = ws_loan_amount
    return ws_loan_interest_rate, ws_monthly_rate, ws_compound_factor, ws_loan_monthly_pmt

def create_amortization_C7(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal, loan_mortgage: bool) -> tuple[List[Decimal], List[Decimal], List[Decimal], List[int], List[Decimal], List[Decimal], int, int]:
    """Creates an amortization schedule."""
    ws_running_balance = ws_loan_amount
    ws_payment_date = "" # Assuming this is current_date equivalent
    ws_payment_month = 1  # Assuming start month
    ws_payment_year = 2024 # Assuming start year
    amort_interest: List[Decimal] = [Decimal(0)] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    amort_principal: List[Decimal] = [Decimal(0)] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    amort_balance: List[Decimal] = [Decimal(0)] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    amort_payment_num: List[int] = [0] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    amort_payment_amt: List[Decimal] = [Decimal(0)] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    amort_escrow: List[Decimal] = [Decimal(0)] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    amort_total_pmt: List[Decimal] = [Decimal(0)] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    for ws_amort_idx in range(1, ws_loan_term_months + 1):
        payment_split = calculate_payment_split_C7(ws_running_balance, ws_monthly_rate, ws_loan_monthly_pmt, ws_property_tax, ws_insurance_premium, ws_pmi_amount, loan_mortgage, ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year)
        amort_interest[ws_amort_idx] = payment_split[0]
        amort_principal[ws_amort_idx] = payment_split[1]
        ws_running_balance = payment_split[2]
        amort_balance[ws_amort_idx] = payment_split[3]
        amort_payment_num[ws_amort_idx] = payment_split[4]
        amort_payment_amt[ws_amort_idx] = payment_split[5]
        amort_escrow[ws_amort_idx] = payment_split[6]
        amort_total_pmt[ws_amort_idx] = payment_split[7]
        ws_payment_month = payment_split[8]
        ws_payment_year = payment_split[9]
    return amort_interest, amort_principal, amort_balance, amort_payment_num, amort_payment_amt, amort_escrow, ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year

def calculate_payment_split_C7(ws_running_balance: Decimal, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal, loan_mortgage: bool, ws_payment_month: int, ws_payment_year: int) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, int, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, int, int]:
    """Calculates the split between interest and principal for a payment."""
    amort_interest = ws_running_balance * ws_monthly_rate
    amort_principal = ws_loan_monthly_pmt - amort_interest
    ws_running_balance -= amort_principal
    amort_balance = ws_running_balance
    ws_amort_idx = 1 # Assuming this is being called in a loop and this is the index
    amort_payment_num = ws_amort_idx
    amort_payment_amt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
    if loan_mortgage:
        amort_escrow = (ws_property_tax + ws_insurance_premium) / 12
        amort_total_pmt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt + amort_escrow + ws_pmi_amount
    else:
        amort_escrow = Decimal(0)
        amort_total_pmt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
    payment_date = advance_payment_date_C7(ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year)
    ws_payment_month = payment_date[0]
    ws_payment_year = payment_date[1]
    return amort_interest, amort_principal, ws_running_balance, amort_balance, amort_payment_num, amort_payment_amt, amort_escrow, amort_total_pmt, ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year

def advance_payment_date_C7(ws_payment_month: int, ws_payment_year: int) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """Advances the payment date by one month."""
    ws_payment_month += 1
    if ws_payment_month > 12:
        ws_payment_month = 1
        ws_payment_year += 1
    return ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year

def finalize_loan_C7(ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_loan_id: str, ws_loan_type: str, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_interest_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal) -> None:
    """Finalizes the loan process."""
    ws_loan_start_date = "" # Replace with current date function
    ws_loan_end_date = ws_loan_start_date + (ws_loan_term_months * 30)
    ws_loan_status = 'A'
    create_loan_record_C7(ws_loan_id, ws_loan_type, ws_loan_amount, ws_loan_interest_rate, ws_loan_monthly_pmt, ws_loan_start_date, ws_loan_status)
    disburse_funds_C7(ws_loan_amount)
    send_confirmation_C7()

    loan_record = f"{loan_rec_id}, {loan_rec_type}, {loan_rec_amount}, {loan_rec_rate}, {loan_rec_payment}, {loan_rec_start}, {loan_rec_status}"
    # Assuming a write function to a file/database
    write_loan_record(loan_record)

def load_portfolio_C7() -> tuple[List[Any], int, str]:
    """Loads an investment portfolio."""
    ws_holding: List[Any] = []
    ws_hold_idx = 1
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    ws_holdings_count = 0

    while ws_hold_idx <= 100 and ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_holding_rec = read_holdings_file()  # Replace with actual file reading
            ws_holding.append(ws_holding_rec)
            ws_hold_idx += 1
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_holdings_count = ws_hold_idx - 1
    return ws_holding, ws_holdings_count, ws_eof_flag

def read_holdings_file() -> Any:
    """Placeholder for reading from file"""
    pass

def update_market_prices_C7(ws_holding: List[Any], ws_holdings_count: int) -> List[Any]:
    """Updates market prices for holdings."""
    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        ws_quote_symbol = get_symbol(ws_holding[ws_hold_idx - 1]) # Assuming holding is a dictionary
        quote = get_quote_C7(ws_quote_symbol)
        ws_quote_price = quote
        update_price(ws_holding[ws_hold_idx - 1], ws_quote_price) # Update list of holdings
    return ws_holding

    """Get symbol of holding"""
    return ""

    """Update price in holding"""

    """Gets a stock quote."""
    quote_request_symbol = ws_quote_symbol
    quote_response = getquote(quote_request_symbol)
    quote_response_status = ""  # Assuming this exists in the quote response
    if quote_response_status == 'OK':
        ws_quote_price = Decimal(0) #assuming quote_last_price exists in response
    else:
        ws_quote_price = Decimal(0)
    return ws_quote_price

def calculate_values_C7(ws_holding: List[Any], ws_holdings_count: int) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates values for a portfolio."""
    ws_total_value = Decimal(0)
    ws_cost_basis = Decimal(0)
    ws_unrealized_gain = Decimal(0)
    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        values = calculate_holding_value_C7(ws_holding[ws_hold_idx - 1])
        ws_total_value += values[0]
        ws_cost_basis += values[1]
        ws_unrealized_gain += values[2]
    return ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain

def calculate_holding_value_C7(holding: Any) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates the value of a holding."""
    hold_shares = 0 #placeholders for now
    hold_current_price = Decimal(0)
    hold_cost_per_share = Decimal(0)
    hold_market_value = hold_shares * hold_current_price
    ws_hold_cost = hold_shares * hold_cost_per_share
    hold_gain_loss = hold_market_value - ws_hold_cost
    if ws_hold_cost > 0:
        hold_pct_change = (hold_gain_loss / ws_hold_cost) * 100
    else:
        hold_pct_change = Decimal(0)
    return hold_market_value, ws_hold_cost, hold_gain_loss

def rebalance_check_C7(ws_holding: List[Any], ws_holdings_count: int) -> None:
    """Checks if a portfolio needs rebalancing."""
    rebalance_data = calculate_current_allocation_C7(ws_holding, ws_holdings_count)
    ws_stocks_pct = rebalance_data[0]
    ws_bonds_pct = rebalance_data[1]
    ws_cash_pct = rebalance_data[2]

    ws_rebalance_needed = compare_to_target_C7(ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct)
    if ws_rebalance_needed == 'Y':
        generate_rebalance_trades_C7(ws_stocks_pct)

def calculate_current_allocation_C7(ws_holding: List[Any], ws_holdings_count: int) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates the current asset allocation."""
    ws_stocks_value = Decimal(0)
    ws_bonds_value = Decimal(0)
    ws_cash_value = Decimal(0)
    ws_total_value = Decimal(0) #temp
    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        hold_type = get_holding_type(ws_holding[ws_hold_idx - 1])  # Replace with actual access
        hold_market_value = get_market_value(ws_holding[ws_hold_idx - 1])

        if hold_type == 'STK':
            ws_stocks_value += hold_market_value
        elif hold_type == 'BND':
            ws_bonds_value += hold_market_value
        elif hold_type == 'CSH':
            ws_cash_value += hold_market_value
    ws_total_value = ws_stocks_value + ws_bonds_value + ws_cash_value
    ws_stocks_pct = (ws_stocks_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_bonds_pct = (ws_bonds_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_cash_pct = (ws_cash_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    return ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct, ws_cash_pct

def get_holding_type(holding: Any) -> str:
    """Get holding type from record"""
    return ""

def get_market_value(holding: Any) -> Decimal:
    """get market value"""
    return Decimal(0)

def compare_to_target_C7(ws_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_bonds_pct: Decimal) -> str:
    """Compares current allocation to target."""
    ws_target_stocks_pct = Decimal(50) #placeholders
    ws_target_bonds_pct = Decimal(30)
    ws_rebalance_needed = 'N'
    ws_stocks_diff = ws_stocks_pct - ws_target_stocks_pct
    ws_bonds_diff = ws_bonds_pct - ws_target_bonds_pct
    if abs(ws_stocks_diff) > 5:
        ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'
    if abs(ws_bonds_diff) > 5:
        ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'
    return ws_rebalance_needed

def generate_rebalance_trades_C7(ws_stocks_pct: Decimal) -> None:
    """Generates rebalance trades."""
    ws_total_value = Decimal(100000) #placeholder
    ws_target_stocks_pct = Decimal(50)
    ws_stocks_diff = ws_stocks_pct - ws_target_stocks_pct
    if ws_stocks_diff > 0:
        ws_sell_amount = ws_total_value * ws_stocks_diff / 100
        create_sell_order_C7(ws_sell_amount)
    else:
        ws_buy_amount = ws_total_value * (0 - ws_stocks_diff) / 100
        create_buy_order_C7(ws_buy_amount)

def trade_execution_C7() -> None:

def validate_order_C7() -> None:
    """Validates a trade order."""
    ws_order_valid = 'Y'
    ws_trade_symbol = "" #placeholder
    ws_reject_reason = ""
    ws_trade_shares = 0 #placeholder
    ws_limit_price = Decimal(0)
    order_limit = False #placeholder
    order_stop_limit = False

    if ws_trade_symbol == "":
        ws_order_valid = 'N'
        ws_reject_reason = 'SYMBOL REQUIRED'
        return
    if ws_trade_shares <= 0:
        ws_order_valid = 'N'
        ws_reject_reason = 'INVALID QUANTITY'
        return
    if order_limit or order_stop_limit:
        if ws_limit_price <= 0:
            ws_order_valid = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'LIMIT PRICE REQUIRED'

def check_funds_shares_C7() -> None:
    """Checks if sufficient funds or shares are available."""
    ws_sufficient_flag = 'Y'
    trade_buy = False #placeholder
    trade_sell = False #placeholder
    ws_trade_shares = 0 #placeholder
    ws_estimated_price = Decimal(0) #placeholder
    ws_available_cash = Decimal(0) #placeholder
    ws_reject_reason = ""
    ws_required_funds = Decimal(0) # Added initialization
    if trade_buy:
        ws_required_funds = ws_trade_shares * ws_estimated_price
        if ws_required_funds > ws_available_cash:
            ws_sufficient_flag = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if trade_sell:
        share_data = check_share_position_C7()
        ws_current_shares = share_data #placeholder
        if ws_current_shares < ws_trade_shares:
            ws_sufficient_flag = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'INSUFFICIENT SHARES'

def check_share_position_C7() -> Decimal:
    """Checks the current share position."""
    ws_current_shares = Decimal(0)
    ws_holding = [] #placeholder
    ws_holdings_count = 0
    ws_trade_symbol = ""
    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        hold_symbol = ""  # Assuming this exists and is accessible

        if hold_symbol == ws_trade_symbol:
            add_to_position = Decimal(0) #placeholder
            ws_current_shares += add_to_position
    return ws_current_shares

def limit_order_C7() -> None:
    """Executes a limit order."""
    trade_buy = False #placeholder
    ws_current_market_price = Decimal(0) #placeholder
    ws_limit_price = Decimal(0)
    ws_trade_status = ""
    ws_executed_price = Decimal(0)
    if trade_buy:
        if ws_current_market_price <= ws_limit_price:
            ws_executed_price = ws_current_market_price
            ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:
            ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'
    else:
        if ws_current_market_price >= ws_limit_price:
            ws_executed_price = ws_current_market_price
            ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:
            ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'

def calc_auto_premium_C8(ws_driver_age: int, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_accidents_3yr: int, ws_violations_3yr: int, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal, ws_accident_surcharge: Decimal, ws_violation_surcharge: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates auto premium."""
    if 6 <= ws_driver_age <= 10:
        ws_base_premium += 100
    else:
        ws_base_premium += 50
    if ws_driver_age < 25:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_accidents_3yr > 0:
        ws_accident_surcharge = ws_accidents_3yr * 200
        ws_base_premium += ws_accident_surcharge
    if ws_violations_3yr > 0:
        ws_violation_surcharge = ws_violations_3yr * 100
        ws_base_premium += ws_violation_surcharge
    ws_annual_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_annual_premium / 12

def calc_home_premium_C8(ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_home_age: int, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_flood_zone: str, ws_security_system: str, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_deductible_credit: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates home premium."""
    ws_base_premium = ws_coverage_amount * Decimal('0.003')
    if 0 <= ws_home_age <= 10:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.9')
    elif 11 <= ws_home_age <= 25:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif 26 <= ws_home_age <= 50:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.2')
    else:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_flood_zone == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_security_system == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.9')
    ws_deductible_credit = ws_deductible / 1000 * 50
    ws_base_premium -= ws_deductible_credit
    if ws_base_premium < 200:
        ws_base_premium = Decimal('200')
    ws_annual_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_annual_premium / 12

def calc_health_premium_C8(ws_insured_age: int, ws_plan_type: str, ws_family_plan: str, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates health premium."""
    ws_base_premium = Decimal('300')
    if 0 <= ws_insured_age <= 18:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.5')
    elif 19 <= ws_insured_age <= 30:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif 31 <= ws_insured_age <= 40:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.3')
    elif 41 <= ws_insured_age <= 50:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.6')
    elif 51 <= ws_insured_age <= 60:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.0')
    else:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.8')
    if ws_plan_type == 'BRONZE':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.8')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'SILVER':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'GOLD':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.3')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'PLATINUM':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.6')
    if ws_family_plan == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.5')
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_annual_premium = ws_monthly_premium * 12

def underwriting_C8() -> None:
    """Performs underwriting."""
    evaluate_risk_factors_C8()
    check_medical_history_C8()
    verify_information_C8()
    determine_decision_C8()

def evaluate_risk_factors_C8(policy_life: bool, policy_auto: bool, ws_bmi: int, ws_smoker_flag: str, ws_hazardous_occupation: str, ws_driver_age: int, ws_accidents_3yr: int, ws_risk_points: int) -> None:
    """Evaluates risk factors."""
    ws_risk_points = 0
    if policy_life:
        if ws_bmi > 30:
            ws_risk_points += 10
        if ws_smoker_flag == 'Y':
            ws_risk_points += 25
        if ws_hazardous_occupation == 'Y':
            ws_risk_points += 15
    if policy_auto:
        if ws_driver_age < 21:
            ws_risk_points += 20
        if ws_accidents_3yr > 1:
            ws_risk_points += 15

def check_medical_history_C8(ws_chronic_conditions: int, ws_recent_hospitalization: str, ws_prescription_count: int, ws_risk_points: int, ws_condition_points: int) -> None:
    """Checks medical history."""
    if ws_chronic_conditions > 0:
        ws_condition_points = ws_chronic_conditions * 5
        ws_risk_points += ws_condition_points
    if ws_recent_hospitalization == 'Y':
        ws_risk_points += 10
    if ws_prescription_count > 5:
        ws_risk_points += 5

def verify_information_C8() -> None:
    """Verifies information."""
    check_fraud_indicators_C8()
    validate_documents_C8()

def check_fraud_indicators_C8(ws_recent_claims: int, ws_address_mismatch: str, ws_risk_points: int, ws_fraud_flag: str) -> None:
    """Checks fraud indicators."""
    if ws_recent_claims > 3:
        ws_risk_points += 20
        ws_fraud_flag = 'Y'
    if ws_address_mismatch == 'Y':
        ws_risk_points += 10

def validate_documents_C8(ws_doc_missing: str, ws_uw_status: str) -> None:
    """Validates documents."""
    if ws_doc_missing == 'Y':
        ws_uw_status = 'PENDING'
    else:
        ws_uw_status = 'COMPLETE'

def determine_decision_C8(ws_risk_points: int, ws_uw_decision: str, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Determines decision."""
    if ws_risk_points > 50:
        ws_uw_decision = 'DECLINE'
    elif ws_risk_points > 30:
        ws_uw_decision = 'SUBSTANDARD'
        ws_annual_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    elif ws_risk_points > 15:
        ws_uw_decision = 'STANDARD'
    else:
        ws_uw_decision = 'PREFERRED'
        ws_annual_premium *= Decimal('0.9')

def issue_policy_C8(ws_uw_decision: str) -> None:
    """Issues policy."""
    if ws_uw_decision != 'DECLINE':
        generate_policy_number_C8()
        create_policy_record_C8()
        set_beneficiaries_C8()
        send_policy_docs_C8()
    else:
        send_decline_letter_C8()

def generate_policy_number_C8(ws_date_part: str, ws_policy_type: str, ws_type_part: str, ws_random_part: int, ws_policy_number: str) -> None:
    """Generates policy number."""
    ws_date_part = "current_date"
    ws_type_part = ws_policy_type
    ws_random_part = int(random() * 99999)
    ws_policy_number = ws_type_part + ws_date_part + str(ws_random_part)

def create_policy_record_C8(ws_policy_record: dict, ws_policy_number: str, ws_policy_type: str, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_effective_date: str, ws_expiration_date: str) -> None:
    """Creates policy record."""
    ws_policy_record = {}
    policy_rec_number = ws_policy_number
    policy_rec_type = ws_policy_type
    policy_rec_coverage = ws_coverage_amount
    policy_rec_premium = ws_annual_premium
    policy_rec_eff_date = ws_effective_date
    policy_rec_exp_date = ws_expiration_date
    policy_rec_status = 'A'
    policy_record = ws_policy_record

def set_beneficiaries_C8(ws_benef_idx: int, ws_policy_number: str, benef_name: List[str], benef_relation: List[str], benef_pct: List[Decimal], ws_beneficiary_rec: dict) -> None:
    """Sets beneficiaries."""
    for ws_benef_idx in range(1, 6):
        if benef_name[ws_benef_idx - 1] != "SPACES":
            ws_beneficiary_rec = {}
            benef_rec_policy = ws_policy_number
            benef_rec_name = benef_name[ws_benef_idx - 1]
            benef_rec_relation = benef_relation[ws_benef_idx - 1]
            benef_rec_pct = benef_pct[ws_benef_idx - 1]
            beneficiary_record = ws_beneficiary_rec

def send_policy_docs_C8(ws_policy_number: str) -> None:
    """Sends policy documents."""
    ws_notif_type = 'policy_issue'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = f'Your policy {ws_policy_number} has been issued'
    send_notification_C8()

def send_decline_letter_C8() -> None:
    """Sends decline letter."""
    ws_notif_type = 'policy_decline'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Regarding your insurance application'
    send_notification_C8()

def claims_handling_C8() -> None:
    """Handles claims."""
    receive_claim_C8()
    validate_claim_C8()
    investigate_claim_C8()
    adjudicate_claim_C8()
    process_payment_C8()

def receive_claim_C8(ws_claim_date: str, ws_claim_status: str) -> None:
    """Receives claim."""
    ws_claim_date = "current_date"
    generate_claim_number_C8()
    ws_claim_status = 'RECEIVED'

def generate_claim_number_C8(ws_date_part: str, ws_random_part: int, ws_claim_number: str) -> None:
    """Generates claim number."""
    ws_date_part = "current_date"
    ws_random_part = int(random() * 99999)
    ws_claim_number = 'CLM' + ws_date_part + str(ws_random_part)

def validate_claim_C8() -> None:
    """Validates claim."""
    check_policy_status_C8()
    check_coverage_C8()
    check_deductible_C8()

def check_policy_status_C8(ws_policy_status: str, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Checks policy status."""
    if ws_policy_status != 'A':
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'POLICY NOT ACTIVE'

def check_coverage_C8(ws_claim_type: str, ws_covered_perils: str, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Checks coverage."""
    if ws_claim_type != ws_covered_perils:
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'NOT COVERED PERIL'

def check_deductible_C8(ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Checks deductible."""
    if ws_claim_amount <= ws_deductible:
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'BELOW DEDUCTIBLE'

def investigate_claim_C8(ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_claim_status: str, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_recent_claims: int, ws_fraud_review: str) -> None:
    """Investigates claim."""
    if ws_claim_amount > 10000:
        ws_claim_status = 'INVESTIGATION'
        assign_adjuster_C8()
    fraud_check_C8(ws_recent_claims, ws_claim_amount, ws_coverage_amount, ws_fraud_review)

def assign_adjuster_C8(ws_adjuster_id: str, ws_notes: str) -> None:
    """Assigns adjuster."""
    ws_adjuster_id = 'ADJ001'
    ws_notes = 'Assigned for investigation'

def fraud_check_C8(ws_recent_claims: int, ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_fraud_review: str) -> None:
    """Checks for fraud."""
    if ws_recent_claims > 2:
        ws_fraud_review = 'Y'
    if ws_claim_amount > ws_coverage_amount * Decimal('0.8'):
        ws_fraud_review = 'Y'

def adjudicate_claim_C8(ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_approved_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Adjudicates claim."""
    if ws_claim_status != 'DENIED':
        ws_approved_amount = ws_claim_amount - ws_deductible
        if ws_approved_amount > ws_coverage_amount:
            ws_approved_amount = ws_coverage_amount
        ws_claim_status = 'APPROVED'

def process_payment_C8(ws_claim_status: str) -> None:
    """Processes payment."""
    if ws_claim_status == 'APPROVED':
        issue_payment_C8()
        update_claim_record_C8()

def issue_payment_C8(ws_claim_number: str, ws_approved_amount: Decimal, ws_payment_record: dict) -> None:
    """Issues payment."""
    ws_payment_record = {}
    pay_rec_claim = ws_claim_number
    pay_rec_amount = ws_approved_amount
    pay_rec_date = "current_date"
    pay_rec_method = 'CHECK'
    payment_record = ws_payment_record

def update_claim_record_C8(ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_close_date: str) -> None:
    """Updates claim record."""
    ws_claim_status = 'PAID'
    ws_claim_close_date = "current_date"
    claim_record = None

def payroll_processing_C8(ws_employee_id: str) -> None:
    """Processes payroll."""
    load_employee_data_C8(ws_employee_id)
    calculate_gross_pay_C8()
    calculate_taxes_C8()
    calculate_deductions_C8()
    calculate_net_pay_C8()
    generate_paystubs_C8()
    process_direct_deposit_C8()

def load_employee_data_C8(ws_employee_id: str, ws_error_msg: str) -> None:
    """Loads employee data."""
    emp_search_key = ws_employee_id
    ws_employee_rec = None
    emp_id = None
    if emp_id != emp_search_key:
        ws_error_msg = 'EMPLOYEE NOT FOUND'
        handle_error_C8()

def calculate_gross_pay_C8(ws_pay_type: str) -> None:
    """Calculates gross pay."""
    if ws_pay_type == 'SALARY':
        calc_salary_pay_C8()
    elif ws_pay_type == 'HOURLY':
        calc_hourly_pay_C8()
    elif ws_pay_type == 'COMMISSION':
        calc_commission_pay_C8()

def calc_salary_pay_C8(ws_annual_salary: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates salary pay."""
    ws_gross_pay = ws_annual_salary / ws_pay_periods

def calc_hourly_pay_C8(ws_hours_worked: Decimal, ws_hourly_rate: Decimal, ws_regular_pay: Decimal, ws_overtime_pay: Decimal, ws_ot_hours: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates hourly pay."""
    if ws_hours_worked <= 40:
        ws_regular_pay = ws_hours_worked * ws_hourly_rate
        ws_overtime_pay = Decimal('0')
    else:
        ws_regular_pay = 40 * ws_hourly_rate
        ws_ot_hours = ws_hours_worked - 40
        ws_overtime_pay = ws_ot_hours * ws_hourly_rate * Decimal('1.5')
    ws_gross_pay = ws_regular_pay + ws_overtime_pay

def calc_commission_pay_C8(ws_base_salary: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_sales_amount: Decimal, ws_commission_rate: Decimal, ws_base_pay: Decimal, ws_commission_pay: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates commission pay."""
    ws_base_pay = ws_base_salary / ws_pay_periods
    ws_commission_pay = ws_sales_amount * ws_commission_rate
    ws_gross_pay = ws_base_pay + ws_commission_pay

def calculate_taxes_C8() -> None:
    """Calculates taxes."""
    calc_federal_tax_C8()
    calc_state_tax_C8()
    calc_local_tax_C8()
    calc_fica_C8()

def calc_federal_tax_C8(ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_exemptions: int, ws_annualized_gross: Decimal, ws_allowance_amount: Decimal, ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal, ws_federal_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates federal tax."""
    ws_annualized_gross = ws_gross_pay * ws_pay_periods
    ws_allowance_amount = ws_exemptions * 4300
    ws_taxable_income = ws_annualized_gross - ws_allowance_amount
    if ws_taxable_income < 0:
        ws_taxable_income = Decimal('0')
    apply_tax_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income, ws_annual_tax)
    ws_federal_tax = ws_annual_tax / ws_pay_periods

def apply_tax_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Applies tax brackets."""
    ws_annual_tax = Decimal('0')
    status_single = False
    status_married_joint = False
    if status_single:
        single_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income, ws_annual_tax)
    elif status_married_joint:
        married_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income, ws_annual_tax)

def single_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates tax for single filers."""
    if ws_taxable_income <= 10275:
        ws_annual_tax = ws_taxable_income * Decimal('0.10')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 41775:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('1027.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 10275) * Decimal('0.12')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 89075:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('4807.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 41775) * Decimal('0.22')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 170050:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('15213.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 89075) * Decimal('0.24')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 215950:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('34647.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 170050) * Decimal('0.32')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 539900:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('49335.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 215950) * Decimal('0.35')
    else:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('162718.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 539900) * Decimal('0.37')

def married_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates tax for married filers."""
    if ws_taxable_income <= 20550:
        ws_annual_tax = ws_taxable_income * Decimal('0.10')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 83550:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('2055.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 20550) * Decimal('0.12')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 178150:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('9615.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 83550) * Decimal('0.22')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 340100:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('30427.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 178150) * Decimal('0.24')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 431900:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('69295.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 340100) * Decimal('0.32')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 647850:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('98671.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 431900) * Decimal('0.35')
    else:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('174253.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 647850) * Decimal('0.37')

def calc_state_tax_C8(ws_state_code: str, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates state tax."""
    if ws_state_code == 'CA':
        ws_state_tax = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.0725')
    elif ws_state_code == 'NY':
        ws_state_tax = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.0685')
    elif ws_state_code == 'TX':
        ws_state_tax = Decimal('0')
    elif ws_state_code == 'FL':
        ws_state_tax = Decimal('0')
    else:
        ws_state_tax = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.05')

def calc_local_tax_C8(ws_local_tax_rate: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_local_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates local tax."""
    if ws_local_tax_rate > 0:
        ws_local_tax = ws_gross_pay * ws_local_tax_rate
    else:
        ws_local_tax = Decimal('0')

def calc_fica_C8(ws_ytd_gross: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_remaining_cap: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_additional_medicare: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates FICA taxes."""
    if ws_ytd_gross < 160200:
        ws_remaining_cap = 160200 - ws_ytd_gross
        if ws_gross_pay <= ws_remaining_cap:
            ws_fica_ss = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.062')
        else:
            ws_fica_ss = ws_remaining_cap * Decimal('0.062')
    else:
        ws_fica_ss = Decimal('0')
    ws_fica_medicare = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.0145')
    if ws_ytd_gross > 200000:
        ws_additional_medicare = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.009')
        ws_fica_medicare += ws_additional_medicare

def calculate_deductions_C8() -> None:
    """Calculates deductions."""
    calc_pre_tax_deductions_C8()
    calc_post_tax_deductions_C8()

def calc_pre_tax_deductions_C8(ws_401k_pct: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_ytd_401k: Decimal, ws_401k_contrib: Decimal, ws_health_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_dental_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_vision_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_hsa_deduct: Decimal, ws_fsa_deduct: Decimal, ws_health_ins: Decimal, ws_dental_ins: Decimal, ws_vision_ins: Decimal, ws_hsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_fsa_contrib: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates pre-tax deductions."""
    if ws_401k_pct > 0:
        ws_401k_contrib = ws_gross_pay * ws_401k_pct / 100
        if ws_ytd_401k + ws_401k_contrib > 22500:
            ws_401k_contrib = 22500 - ws_ytd_401k
            if ws_401k_contrib < 0:
                ws_401k_contrib = Decimal('0')
    ws_health_ins = ws_health_ins_deduct
    ws_dental_ins = ws_dental_ins_deduct
    ws_vision_ins = ws_vision_ins_deduct
    ws_hsa_contrib = ws_hsa_deduct
    ws_fsa_contrib = ws_fsa_deduct

def calc_post_tax_deductions_C8(ws_life_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_disability_deduct: Decimal, ws_union_dues_amt: Decimal, ws_garnishment_amt: Decimal, ws_life_ins: Decimal, ws_disability_ins: Decimal, ws_union_dues: Decimal, ws_garnishment: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates post-tax deductions."""
    ws_life_ins = ws_life_ins_deduct
    ws_disability_ins = ws_disability_deduct
    ws_union_dues = ws_union_dues_amt
    ws_garnishment = ws_garnishment_amt

def calculate_net_pay_C8(ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_federal_tax: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal, ws_local_tax: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_health_ins: Decimal, ws_dental_ins: Decimal, ws_vision_ins: Decimal, ws_401k_contrib: Decimal, ws_hsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_fsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_life_ins: Decimal, ws_disability_ins: Decimal, ws_union_dues: Decimal, ws_garnishment: Decimal, ws_other_deduct: Decimal, ws_total_deductions: Decimal, ws_net_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates net pay."""
    ws_total_deductions = (ws_federal_tax + ws_state_tax + ws_local_tax + 0  # TODO
                             ws_fica_ss + ws_fica_medicare + 0  # TODO
                             ws_health_ins + ws_dental_ins + ws_vision_ins + 0  # TODO
                             ws_401k_contrib + ws_hsa_contrib + ws_fsa_contrib + 0  # TODO
                             ws_life_ins + ws_disability_ins + 0  # TODO
                             ws_union_dues + ws_garnishment + ws_other_deduct)
    ws_net_pay = ws_gross_pay - ws_total_deductions
    update_ytd_totals_C8(ws_gross_pay, ws_federal_tax, ws_state_tax, ws_fica_ss, ws_fica_medicare, ws_net_pay, ws_401k_contrib)

def update_ytd_totals_C8(ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_federal_tax: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_net_pay: Decimal, ws_401k_contrib: Decimal) -> None:
    """Updates year-to-date totals."""
    ws_ytd_gross = Decimal('0.0')
    ws_ytd_fed_tax = Decimal('0.0')
    ws_ytd_state_tax = Decimal('0.0')
    ws_ytd_fica = Decimal('0.0')
    ws_ytd_net = Decimal('0.0')
    ws_ytd_401k = Decimal('0.0')
    ws_ytd_gross += ws_gross_pay
    ws_ytd_fed_tax += ws_federal_tax
    ws_ytd_state_tax += ws_state_tax
    ws_ytd_fica += ws_fica_ss
    ws_ytd_fica += ws_fica_medicare
    ws_ytd_net += ws_net_pay
    ws_ytd_401k += ws_401k_contrib

def generate_paystubs_C8(ws_employee_id: str, ws_pay_period: str, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_federal_tax: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_net_pay: Decimal, ws_ytd_gross: Decimal, ws_ytd_net: Decimal, ws_paystub_record: dict) -> None:
    """Generates paystubs."""
    ws_paystub_record = {}
    stub_emp_id = ws_employee_id
    stub_pay_period = ws_pay_period
    stub_gross = ws_gross_pay
    stub_fed_tax = ws_federal_tax
    stub_state_tax = ws_state_tax
    stub_ss = ws_fica_ss
    stub_medicare = ws_fica_medicare
    stub_net = ws_net_pay
    stub_ytd_gross = ws_ytd_gross
    stub_ytd_net = ws_ytd_net
    paystub_record = ws_paystub_record

    if ws_routing_number == "SPACES":

    elif ws_account_number == "SPACES":

def calculate_next_run_date_C10(ws_last_run_date: Any, ws_next_run_date: Any, run_frequency: str) -> None:
    """Calculates the next run date based on the specified frequency."""
    if run_frequency == 'DAILY':
        ws_next_run_date = function_integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 1
    elif run_frequency == 'WEEKLY':
        ws_next_run_date = function_integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 7
    elif run_frequency == 'MONTHLY':
        ws_next_run_date = function_integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 30
    elif run_frequency == 'QUARTERLY':
        ws_next_run_date = function_integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 90
    elif run_frequency == 'YEARLY':
        ws_next_run_date = function_integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 365

def function_integer_of_date_C10(date: Any) -> int:
    """Dummy function for integer of date."""
    pass

def data_analytics_and_reporting_procedures_C10() -> None:

    export_data_C10()

def collect_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects various metrics."""
    collect_transaction_metrics_C10()
    collect_customer_metrics_C10()
    collect_performance_metrics_C10()

def collect_transaction_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects transaction metrics."""
    global ws_total_trans_amount_C10, ws_total_trans_count_C10, ws_avg_trans_amount_C10, ws_eof_flag_C10
    ws_total_trans_amount_C10 = 0
    ws_total_trans_count_C10 = 0
    ws_avg_trans_amount_C10 = 0
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C10 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_trans_rec_C10 = transaction_file_read_C10()
            ws_total_trans_count_C10 += 1
            ws_total_trans_amount_C10 += ws_trans_rec_C10.trans_amount
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'Y'
    if ws_total_trans_count_C10 > 0:
        ws_avg_trans_amount_C10 = ws_total_trans_amount_C10 / ws_total_trans_count_C10
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'

def transaction_file_read_C10():
    """Dummy transaction file reader."""
    pass

def collect_customer_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects customer metrics."""
    global ws_active_customers_C10, ws_new_customers_C10, ws_churned_customers_C10, ws_eof_flag_C10
    ws_active_customers_C10 = 0
    ws_new_customers_C10 = 0
    ws_churned_customers_C10 = 0
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C10 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_cust_rec_C10 = customer_file_read_C10()
            if ws_cust_rec_C10.cust_status == 'A':
                ws_active_customers_C10 += 1
            if ws_cust_rec_C10.cust_open_date >= ws_period_start_C10:
                ws_new_customers_C10 += 1
            if ws_cust_rec_C10.cust_close_date >= ws_period_start_C10:
                ws_churned_customers_C10 += 1
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'

def customer_file_read_C10():
    """Dummy customer file reader."""
    pass

def collect_performance_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects performance metrics."""
    global ws_response_time_total_C10, ws_response_count_C10, ws_avg_response_time_C10, ws_eof_flag_C10
    ws_response_time_total_C10 = 0
    ws_response_count_C10 = 0
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C10 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_perf_rec_C10 = perf_log_file_read_C10()
            ws_response_time_total_C10 += ws_perf_rec_C10.perf_response_time
            ws_response_count_C10 += 1
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'Y'
    if ws_response_count_C10 > 0:
        ws_avg_response_time_C10 = ws_response_time_total_C10 / ws_response_count_C10
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'

    """Dummy performance log file reader."""
    pass

def daily_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """Performs daily data aggregation."""
    global ws_daily_summary_C10
    ws_daily_summary_C10 = {}
    ws_daily_summary_C10['daily_date'] = ws_process_date_C10
    ws_daily_summary_C10['daily_trans_count'] = ws_total_trans_count_C10
    ws_daily_summary_C10['daily_trans_amount'] = ws_total_trans_amount_C10
    ws_daily_summary_C10['daily_deposits'] = ws_total_deposits_C10
    ws_daily_summary_C10['daily_withdrawals'] = ws_total_withdrawals_C10
    write_daily_summary_record_C10(ws_daily_summary_C10)

    """Dummy write daily summary."""
