def weekly_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """Performs weekly data aggregation."""
    if ws_day_of_week_C10 == 7:
        global ws_weekly_summary_C10
        ws_weekly_summary_C10 = {}
        ws_weekly_summary_C10['weekly_week'] = ws_week_number_C10
        sum_week_data_C10()
        write_weekly_summary_record_C10(ws_weekly_summary_C10)

def write_weekly_summary_record_C10(record: dict) -> None:
    """Dummy write weekly summary record."""
    pass

def sum_week_data_C10() -> None:
    """Sums weekly data."""
    global ws_weekly_summary_C10
    ws_weekly_summary_C10['weekly_trans_count'] = 0
    ws_weekly_summary_C10['weekly_trans_amount'] = 0
    for _ in range(7):
        ws_weekly_summary_C10['weekly_trans_count'] += ws_daily_summary_C10['daily_trans_count']
        ws_weekly_summary_C10['weekly_trans_amount'] += ws_daily_summary_C10['daily_trans_amount']

def monthly_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """Performs monthly data aggregation."""
    if ws_end_of_month_C10 == 'Y':
        global ws_monthly_summary_C10
        ws_monthly_summary_C10 = {}
        ws_monthly_summary_C10['monthly_month'] = ws_curr_month_C10
        ws_monthly_summary_C10['monthly_year'] = ws_curr_year_C10
        sum_month_data_C10()
        write_monthly_summary_record_C10(ws_monthly_summary_C10)

def write_monthly_summary_record_C10(record: dict) -> None:
    """Dummy write monthly summary record."""
    pass

def sum_month_data_C10() -> None:
    """Sums monthly data."""
    global ws_monthly_summary_C10, ws_eof_flag_C10
    ws_monthly_summary_C10['monthly_trans_count'] = 0
    ws_monthly_summary_C10['monthly_trans_amount'] = 0
    ws_monthly_summary_C10['monthly_new_accounts'] = 0
    ws_monthly_summary_C10['monthly_closed_accounts'] = 0
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C10 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec_C10 = daily_summary_file_read_C10()
            if ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_month == ws_curr_month_C10:
                ws_monthly_summary_C10['monthly_trans_count'] += ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_trans_count
                ws_monthly_summary_C10['monthly_trans_amount'] += ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_trans_amount
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'

    """Dummy daily summary file reader."""
    pass

def calculate_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates Key Performance Indicators (KPIs)."""
    calc_financial_kpi_C10()
    calc_operational_kpi_C10()
    calc_customer_kpi_C10()

def calc_financial_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates financial KPIs."""
    global ws_roa_C10, ws_roe_C10, ws_nim_C10
    if ws_total_assets_C10 > 0:
        ws_roa_C10 = (ws_net_income_C10 / ws_total_assets_C10) * 100
    if ws_total_equity_C10 > 0:
        ws_roe_C10 = (ws_net_income_C10 / ws_total_equity_C10) * 100
    if ws_interest_expense_C10 > 0:
        ws_nim_C10 = ((ws_interest_income_C10 - ws_interest_expense_C10) / ws_earning_assets_C10) * 100

def calc_operational_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates operational KPIs."""
    global ws_error_rate_C10, ws_sla_compliance_C10, ws_first_call_resolution_C10
    if ws_total_trans_count_C10 > 0:
        ws_error_rate_C10 = (ws_error_count_C10 / ws_total_trans_count_C10) * 100
    ws_sla_compliance_C10 = (ws_within_sla_count_C10 / ws_total_cases_C10) * 100
    ws_first_call_resolution_C10 = (ws_fcr_count_C10 / ws_total_calls_C10) * 100

def calc_customer_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates customer KPIs."""
    global ws_churn_rate_C10, ws_acquisition_cost_C10, ws_lifetime_value_C10
    if ws_active_customers_C10 > 0:
        ws_churn_rate_C10 = (ws_churned_customers_C10 / ws_active_customers_C10) * 100
    ws_acquisition_cost_C10 = ws_marketing_spend_C10 / ws_new_customers_C10
    ws_lifetime_value_C10 = ws_avg_revenue_per_customer_C10 * ws_avg_customer_tenure_C10

    ws_csv_header_C10 = 'Date,TransCount,TransAmount,Deposits,Withdrawals'
    csv_record_write_C10(ws_csv_header_C10)
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C10 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec_C10 = daily_summary_file_read_C10()
            ws_csv_line_C10 = f"{ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_date},{ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_trans_count},{ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_trans_amount},{ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_deposits},{ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_withdrawals}"
            csv_record_write_C10(ws_csv_line_C10)
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'Y'
    csv_export_file_close_C10()
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'

    if ws_first_record_C10 == 'Y':

    else:
        ws_json_comma_C10 = ''
        ws_first_record_C10 = 'Y'
    ws_json_line_C10 = f'{ws_json_comma_C10}{{"date":"{ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_date}","transCount":{ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_trans_count},"transAmount":{ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_trans_amount}}}'
    json_record_write_C10(ws_json_line_C10)

def account_maintenance_C10() -> None:
    """Performs account maintenance procedures."""
    dormant_account_check_C10()
    escheatment_processing_C10()
    account_closure_C10()
    account_reactivation_C10()

def dormant_account_check_C10() -> None:
    """Checks for dormant accounts."""
    global ws_eof_flag_C10
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C10 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_account_rec_C10 = account_file_read_C10()
            check_activity_C10()
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'

def account_file_read_C10():
    """Dummy account file reader."""
    pass

def check_activity_C10() -> None:
    """Checks account activity."""
    global acct_status_C10
    ws_days_inactive_C10 = function_integer_of_date_C10(ws_process_date_C10) - function_integer_of_date_C10(acct_last_activity_C10)
    if ws_days_inactive_C10 > 365:
        acct_status_C10 = 'D'
        mark_dormant_C10()

def mark_dormant_C10() -> None:
    """Marks an account as dormant."""
    global acct_status_desc_C10, acct_dormant_date_C10
    acct_status_desc_C10 = 'DORMANT'
    acct_dormant_date_C10 = ws_process_date_C10
    rewrite_account_record_C10()
    send_dormant_notice_C10()

def rewrite_account_record_C10() -> None:
    """Dummy rewrite account record."""
    pass

def send_dormant_notice_C10() -> None:
    """Sends a dormant account notice."""
    global ws_notif_type_C10, ws_notif_channel_C10, ws_notif_subject_C10
    ws_notif_type_C10 = 'dormant_notice'
    ws_notif_channel_C10 = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject_C10 = 'Important: Your account is dormant'
    send_notification_C10()

def send_notification_C10() -> None:
    """Dummy send notification procedure."""
    pass

def escheatment_processing_C10() -> None:
    """Processes escheatment for dormant accounts."""
    global ws_eof_flag_C10
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C10 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_account_rec_C10 = account_file_read_C10()
            if acct_status_C10 == 'D':
                check_escheatment_C10()
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'

def check_escheatment_C10() -> None:
    """Checks if an account is eligible for escheatment."""
    ws_dormant_years_C10 = (function_integer_of_date_C10(ws_process_date_C10) - function_integer_of_date_C10(acct_dormant_date_C10)) / 365
    if ws_dormant_years_C10 >= ws_escheat_years_C10:
        escheat_account_C10()

def escheat_account_C10() -> None:
    """Escheats an account."""
    global acct_status_C10, ws_escheat_amount_C10, acct_balance_C10
    acct_status_C10 = 'E'
    ws_escheat_amount_C10 = acct_balance_C10
    acct_balance_C10 = 0
    create_escheat_record_C10()
    rewrite_account_record_C10()

def create_escheat_record_C10() -> None:
    """Creates an escheat record."""
    global ws_escheat_record_C10
    ws_escheat_record_C10 = {}
    ws_escheat_record_C10['escheat_account'] = acct_id_C10
    ws_escheat_record_C10['escheat_amount'] = ws_escheat_amount_C10
    ws_escheat_record_C10['escheat_date'] = ws_process_date_C10
    ws_escheat_record_C10['escheat_owner'] = acct_owner_name_C10
    ws_escheat_record_C10['escheat_address'] = acct_owner_address_C10
    write_escheat_record_C10(ws_escheat_record_C10)

def write_escheat_record_C10(record: dict) -> None:
    """Dummy write escheat record."""
    pass

def account_closure_C10() -> None:
    """Processes account closures."""
    if ws_close_request_C10 == 'Y':
        validate_closure_C10()
        if ws_closure_valid_C10 == 'Y':
            process_closure_C10()
        else:
            reject_closure_C10()

def validate_closure_C10() -> None:
    """Validates an account closure request."""
    global ws_closure_valid_C10, ws_closure_reject_C10
    ws_closure_valid_C10 = 'Y'
    if acct_balance_C10 < 0:
        ws_closure_valid_C10 = 'N'
        ws_closure_reject_C10 = 'NEGATIVE BALANCE'
    if acct_pending_trans_C10 > 0:
        ws_closure_valid_C10 = 'N'
        ws_closure_reject_C10 = 'PENDING TRANSACTIONS'
    if acct_loan_link_C10 != ' ':
        ws_closure_valid_C10 = 'N'
        ws_closure_reject_C10 = 'LINKED LOAN EXISTS'

def process_closure_C10() -> None:
    """Processes an account closure."""
    global acct_status_C10, acct_close_date_C10
    ws_final_balance_C10 = acct_balance_C10
    disburse_balance_C10()
    acct_status_C10 = 'C'
    acct_close_date_C10 = ws_process_date_C10
    rewrite_account_record_C10()
    archive_account_C10()

def disburse_balance_C10() -> None:
    """Disburses the remaining account balance."""
    if ws_final_balance_C10 > 0:
        ws_check_record_C10 = {}
        ws_check_record_C10['check_from_account'] = acct_id_C10
        ws_check_record_C10['check_amount'] = ws_final_balance_C10
        ws_check_record_C10['check_memo'] = 'ACCOUNT CLOSURE'
        ws_check_record_C10['check_payee'] = acct_owner_name_C10
        write_check_record_C10(ws_check_record_C10)

def write_check_record_C10(record: dict) -> None:
    """Dummy write check record."""
    pass

def archive_account_C10() -> None:
    """Archives the closed account."""
    global ws_archive_record_C10
    ws_archive_record_C10 = {}
    ws_archive_record_C10['archive_account_data'] = ws_account_rec_C10
    ws_archive_record_C10['archive_date'] = ws_process_date_C10
    ws_archive_record_C10['archive_retention'] = function_integer_of_date_C10(ws_process_date_C10) + 2555
    write_archive_record_C10(ws_archive_record_C10)

def write_archive_record_C10(record: dict) -> None:
    """Dummy write archive record."""
    pass

def reject_closure_C10() -> None:
    """Rejects an account closure request."""
    global ws_notif_type_C10, ws_notif_channel_C10, ws_notif_subject_C10
    ws_notif_type_C10 = 'closure_reject'
    ws_notif

def card_blocking_C11() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global CARD_STATUS, WS_BLOCK_REASON, CARD_BLOCK_REASON, WS_PROCESS_DATE, CARD_BLOCK_DATE, CARD_RECORD, WS_CARD_RECORD, WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_BODY
    CARD_STATUS = 'B'
    CARD_BLOCK_REASON  = None  # TODO: was WS_BLOCK_REASON
    CARD_BLOCK_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    # REWRITE card_record FROM ws_card_record
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'card_blocked'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'SMS'
    WS_NOTIF_BODY = 'Your card has been blocked: ' + WS_BLOCK_REASON
    send_notification_C11()

def wire_transfer_C11() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    validate_wire_request_C11()
    if WS_WIRE_VALID == 'Y':
        ofac_screening_C11()
        if WS_OFAC_CLEAR == 'Y':
            process_wire_C11()
            send_confirmation_C11()
        else:
            reject_wire_C11()

def validate_wire_request_C11() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global WS_WIRE_VALID, WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT, WS_CTR_REQUIRED
    WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT == " ":
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000:
        WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y'

def ofac_screening_C11() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global WS_OFAC_CLEAR, WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME, OFAC_SEARCH_NAME, OFAC_REQUEST, OFAC_RESPONSE, OFAC_MATCH_FOUND, OFAC_MATCH_SCORE, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK, OFAC_SEARCH_BANK
    WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'Y'
    OFAC_SEARCH_NAME  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
    # CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING ofac_request ofac_response
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'OFAC MATCH'
    OFAC_SEARCH_BANK  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK
    # CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING ofac_request ofac_response
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BANK OFAC MATCH'

def process_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    debit_originator_C11()
    create_wire_message_C11()
    transmit_wire_C11()
    record_wire_C11()

def debit_originator_C11() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_WIRE_FEE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_FEE
    update_account_C11()

def transmit_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE, SWIFT_STATUS, WS_WIRE_STATUS
    # CALL 'SWIFTSEND' USING ws_swift_message ws_swift_response
    if SWIFT_STATUS == 'ACK':
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'SENT'
    else:
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'FAILED'
        reverse_debit_C11()

    # INITIALIZE ws_wire_record
    WIRE_REF  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_REF
    WIRE_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WIRE_STATUS  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_STATUS
    WIRE_FROM_ACCT = WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT
    WIRE_TO_ACCT = WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT
    WIRE_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    # WRITE wire_record FROM ws_wire_record

def reverse_debit_C11() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_WIRE_FEE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_FEE
    update_account_C11()

def receive_ach_file_C11() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ACH_INPUT_FILE, WS_ACH_FILE_HEADER, ACH_FILE_ID, WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE, ACH_CREATION_DATE, WS_ACH_FILE_DATE, ACH_ENTRY_COUNT, WS_EXPECTED_ENTRIES
    # OPEN INPUT ach_input_file
    # READ ach_input_file INTO ws_ach_file_header
    ACH_FILE_ID  = None  # TODO: was WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE
    ACH_CREATION_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_ACH_FILE_DATE

def validate_ach_entries_C11() -> None:
    """TODO"""
    global WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID, ACH_ROUTING, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE, ACH_ACCOUNT, ACH_AMOUNT, WS_VALID_ENTRIES, WS_INVALID_ENTRIES
    WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'Y'
    if not ACH_ROUTING.isnumeric():
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R03'
    if ACH_ACCOUNT == " ":
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
    if ACH_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R06'
    if WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID == 'Y':
        WS_VALID_ENTRIES += 1
    else:
        WS_INVALID_ENTRIES += 1

def process_ach_credits_C11() -> None:
    """TODO"""
    global ACH_ACCOUNT, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, ACH_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_CREDITS_POSTED, WS_TOTAL_CREDITS, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    search_account_C11()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
        update_account_C11()
        WS_CREDITS_POSTED += 1
        WS_TOTAL_CREDITS += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
    else:
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
        create_return_entry_C11()

def process_ach_debits_C11() -> None:
    """TODO"""
    global ACH_ACCOUNT, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, ACH_AMOUNT, WS_DEBITS_POSTED, WS_TOTAL_DEBITS, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    search_account_C11()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        if WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE >= ACH_AMOUNT:
            WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
            update_account_C11()
            WS_DEBITS_POSTED += 1
            WS_TOTAL_DEBITS += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
        else:
            WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R01'
            create_return_entry_C11()
    else:
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
        create_return_entry_C11()

def generate_ach_return_C11() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    if WS_RETURN_COUNT > 0:
        create_return_file_C11()

def create_return_entry_C11() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global WS_ACH_RETURN_ENTRY, ACH_TRACE_NUMBER, RETURN_ORIG_TRACE, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE, RETURN_CODE, ACH_AMOUNT, RETURN_AMOUNT, ACH_ACCOUNT, RETURN_ACCOUNT, WS_RETURN_COUNT, ACH_RETURN_RECORD
    # INITIALIZE ws_ach_return_entry

    RETURN_CODE  = None  # TODO: was WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    RETURN_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
    RETURN_ACCOUNT  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    WS_RETURN_COUNT += 1
    # WRITE ach_return_record FROM ws_ach_return_entry

def create_return_file_C11() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ACH_RETURN_FILE
    # OPEN OUTPUT ach_return_file
    write_return_header_C11()
    write_return_entries_C11()
    write_return_trailer_C11()
    # CLOSE ach_return_file

    """Brief description."""

        # WRITE ach_return_record FROM ws_return_entry(ws_return_idx)
        WS_RETURN_IDX += 1

    """Brief description."""

    generate_account_summary_C11()
    generate_transaction_detail_C11()
    calculate_statement_totals_C11()
    format_statement_C11()
    deliver_statement_C11()

def validate_stop_request_C12() -> None:
    """Validates stop request."""
    global WS_STOP_VALID, WS_CHECK_NUMBER, WS_STOP_REJECT, WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED
    WS_STOP_VALID = 'Y'
    if WS_CHECK_NUMBER == 0:
        WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
        WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'
    if WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED == 'Y':
        WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
        WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'

def create_stop_order_C12() -> None:
    """Creates stop order."""
    global WS_STOP_RECORD, ACCT_ID, STOP_ACCOUNT, WS_CHECK_NUMBER, STOP_CHECK_NUMBER, WS_CHECK_AMOUNT, STOP_AMOUNT, WS_PAYEE_NAME, STOP_PAYEE, WS_PROCESS_DATE, STOP_EFFECTIVE_DATE, STOP_EXPIRY_DATE, STOP_STATUS, STOP_RECORD
    WS_STOP_RECORD = WsStopRecordC12()
    STOP_ACCOUNT  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_ID
    STOP_CHECK_NUMBER  = None  # TODO: was WS_CHECK_NUMBER
    STOP_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_CHECK_AMOUNT
    STOP_PAYEE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PAYEE_NAME
    STOP_EFFECTIVE_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    STOP_EXPIRY_DATE = WS_PROCESS_DATE + 180
    STOP_STATUS = 'A'
    STOP_RECORD = StopRecordC12()

def apply_stop_fee_C12() -> None:
    """Applies stop fee."""
    global WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_CHECK_NUMBER, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE
    update_account_C12()
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'stop_payment'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Stop payment placed on check # {WS_CHECK_NUMBER}'
    send_notification_C12()

def safe_deposit_box_C12() -> None:
    """Processes safe deposit box procedures."""
    box_rental_C12()
    box_access_C12()
    box_drilling_C12()
    box_billing_C12()

def box_rental_C12() -> None:
    """Handles box rental requests."""
    global WS_RENTAL_REQUEST, WS_BOX_AVAILABLE
    if WS_RENTAL_REQUEST == 'Y':
        check_availability_C12()
        if WS_BOX_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
            assign_box_C12()
            create_rental_agreement_C12()

def check_availability_C12() -> None:
    """Checks box availability."""
    global WS_BOX_AVAILABLE, WS_BOX_IDX, WS_TOTAL_BOXES, BOX_STATUS, BOX_SIZE, WS_REQUESTED_SIZE, WS_ASSIGNED_BOX
    WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'N'
    WS_BOX_IDX = 1
    while WS_BOX_IDX <= WS_TOTAL_BOXES:
        if BOX_STATUS[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'A':
            if BOX_SIZE[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == WS_REQUESTED_SIZE:
                WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
                WS_ASSIGNED_BOX  = None  # TODO: was WS_BOX_IDX
                break
        WS_BOX_IDX += 1

def assign_box_C12() -> None:
    """Assigns a box to a renter."""
    global WS_ASSIGNED_BOX, BOX_STATUS, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, BOX_RENTER, WS_PROCESS_DATE, BOX_RENTAL_DATE
    BOX_STATUS[WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1] = 'R'
    BOX_RENTER[WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1]  = None  # TODO: was WS_CUSTOMER_ID
    BOX_RENTAL_DATE[WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1]  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE

def create_rental_agreement_C12() -> None:
    """Creates a rental agreement."""
    global WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT, WS_ASSIGNED_BOX, RENTAL_BOX_NUMBER, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, RENTAL_CUSTOMER, WS_PROCESS_DATE, RENTAL_START_DATE, RENTAL_ANNUAL_FEE, WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE, WS_REQUESTED_SIZE, RENTAL_RECORD
    WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT = WsRentalAgreementC12()
    RENTAL_BOX_NUMBER  = None  # TODO: was WS_ASSIGNED_BOX
    RENTAL_CUSTOMER  = None  # TODO: was WS_CUSTOMER_ID
    RENTAL_START_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    RENTAL_ANNUAL_FEE = WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE[int(WS_REQUESTED_SIZE) - 1]
    RENTAL_RECORD = RentalRecordC12()

def box_access_C12() -> None:
    """Handles box access requests."""
    global WS_ACCESS_REQUEST, WS_RENTER_VERIFIED
    if WS_ACCESS_REQUEST == 'Y':
        verify_renter_C12()
        if WS_RENTER_VERIFIED == 'Y':
            log_access_C12()
            escort_to_vault_C12()

def verify_renter_C12() -> None:
    """Verifies the renter."""
    global WS_RENTER_VERIFIED, WS_BOX_NUMBER, BOX_RENTER, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, WS_ID_VERIFIED, WS_KEY_VERIFIED
    WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'N'
    if BOX_RENTER[WS_BOX_NUMBER - 1] == WS_CUSTOMER_ID:
        if WS_ID_VERIFIED == 'Y':
            if WS_KEY_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'Y'

def log_access_C12() -> None:
    """Logs access to the box."""
    global WS_ACCESS_LOG, WS_BOX_NUMBER, ACCESS_BOX_NUMBER, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, ACCESS_CUSTOMER, WS_PROCESS_DATE, ACCESS_DATE, ACCESS_TIME, ACCESS_TYPE, ACCESS_LOG_RECORD
    WS_ACCESS_LOG = WsAccessLogC12()
    ACCESS_BOX_NUMBER  = None  # TODO: was WS_BOX_NUMBER
    ACCESS_CUSTOMER  = None  # TODO: was WS_CUSTOMER_ID
    ACCESS_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    ACCESS_TIME = "CURRENT_TIME"
    ACCESS_TYPE = 'ENTRY'
    ACCESS_LOG_RECORD = AccessLogRecordC12()

def escort_to_vault_C12() -> None:
    """Grants vault access."""
    global WS_DISPLAY_MSG
    WS_DISPLAY_MSG = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED'
    print(WS_DISPLAY_MSG)

def box_drilling_C12() -> None:
    """Handles box drilling requests."""
    global WS_DRILLING_REQUEST, WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED
    if WS_DRILLING_REQUEST == 'Y':
        validate_drilling_auth_C12()
        if WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED == 'Y':
            schedule_drilling_C12()
            notify_renter_C12()

def validate_drilling_auth_C12() -> None:
    """Validates drilling authorization."""
    global WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED, WS_RENT_DELINQUENT_MONTHS, WS_COURT_ORDER, WS_DECEASED_RENTER, WS_EXECUTOR_VERIFIED
    WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'N'
    if WS_RENT_DELINQUENT_MONTHS >= 12:
        WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'
    if WS_COURT_ORDER == 'Y':
        WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'
    if WS_DECEASED_RENTER == 'Y':
        if WS_EXECUTOR_VERIFIED == 'Y':
            WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'

def schedule_drilling_C12() -> None:
    """Schedules box drilling."""
    global WS_DRILLING_RECORD, WS_BOX_NUMBER, DRILL_BOX_NUMBER, WS_DRILLING_REASON, DRILL_REASON, WS_PROCESS_DATE, DRILL_SCHEDULED_DATE, DRILLING_RECORD
    WS_DRILLING_RECORD = WsDrillingRecordC12()
    DRILL_BOX_NUMBER  = None  # TODO: was WS_BOX_NUMBER
    DRILL_REASON  = None  # TODO: was WS_DRILLING_REASON
    DRILL_SCHEDULED_DATE = WS_PROCESS_DATE + 30
    DRILLING_RECORD = DrillingRecordC12()

def notify_renter_C12() -> None:
    """Notifies the renter about the drilling."""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'box_drilling'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box'
    send_notification_C12()

def box_billing_C12() -> None:
    """Processes box billing."""
    global WS_BOX_IDX, WS_TOTAL_BOXES, BOX_STATUS, BOX_RENEWAL_DUE
    WS_BOX_IDX = 1
    while WS_BOX_IDX <= WS_TOTAL_BOXES:
        if BOX_STATUS[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'R':
            if BOX_RENEWAL_DUE[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'Y':
                charge_annual_fee_C12()
        WS_BOX_IDX += 1

def charge_annual_fee_C12() -> None:
    """Charges the annual fee for the box."""
    global WS_BOX_IDX, BOX_RENTER, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, BOX_ANNUAL_FEE, WS_FEE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL
    WS_CUSTOMER_ID = BOX_RENTER[WS_BOX_IDX - 1]
    WS_FEE_AMOUNT = BOX_ANNUAL_FEE[WS_BOX_IDX - 1]
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_FEE_AMOUNT
    update_account_C12()
    BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] = BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] + 10000

def merchant_services_C12() -> None:
    """Processes merchant services."""
    process_authorization_C12()
    capture_transaction_C12()
    process_settlement_C12()
    handle_chargeback_C12()

def process_authorization_C12() -> None:
    """Processes authorization."""
    global WS_CARD_VALID, WS_FRAUD_APPROVED, WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE
    validate_card_C12()
    if WS_CARD_VALID == 'Y':
        check_fraud_score_C12()
        if WS_FRAUD_APPROVED == 'Y':
            check_available_credit_C12()
            if WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
                approve_auth_C12()
            else:
                decline_auth_C12()
        else:
            decline_auth_C12()
    else:
        decline_auth_C12()

def validate_card_C12() -> None:
    """Validates card information."""
    global WS_CARD_VALID, WS_LUHN_VALID, WS_NOT_EXPIRED, WS_CVV_VALID
    WS_CARD_VALID = 'N'
    check_luhn_C12()
    if WS_LUHN_VALID == 'Y':
        check_expiry_C12()
        if WS_NOT_EXPIRED == 'Y':
            check_cvv_C12()
            if WS_CVV_VALID == 'Y':
                WS_CARD_VALID = 'Y'

def check_luhn_C12() -> None:
    """Checks the Luhn algorithm."""
    global WS_LUHN_SUM, WS_LUHN_IDX, WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, WS_LUHN_DIGIT, WS_LUHN_VALID
    WS_LUHN_SUM = 0
    WS_LUHN_IDX = 16
    while WS_LUHN_IDX >= 1:
        WS_LUHN_DIGIT = int(WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER[WS_LUHN_IDX - 1])
        if (17 - WS_LUHN_IDX) % 2 == 0:
            WS_LUHN_DIGIT *= 2
            if WS_LUHN_DIGIT > 9:
                WS_LUHN_DIGIT -= 9
        WS_LUHN_SUM += None  # TODO: was WS_LUHN_DIGIT
        WS_LUHN_IDX -= 1
    if WS_LUHN_SUM % 10 == 0:
        WS_LUHN_VALID = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_LUHN_VALID = 'N'

def check_expiry_C12() -> None:
    """Checks the expiry date."""
    global WS_AUTH_EXPIRY_DATE, WS_PROCESS_DATE, WS_NOT_EXPIRED
    if WS_AUTH_EXPIRY_DATE >= WS_PROCESS_DATE:
        WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'N'

def check_cvv_C12() -> None:
    """Checks the CVV."""
    global WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, WS_AUTH_CVV, WS_CVV_RESULT, WS_CVV_VALID
    WS_CVV_RESULT = "M"
    if WS_CVV_RESULT == 'M':
        WS_CVV_VALID = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_CVV_VALID = 'N'

def check_fraud_score_C12() -> None:
    """Checks the fraud score."""
    global WS_AUTH_REQUEST, WS_FRAUD_RESPONSE, FRAUD_SCORE, WS_FRAUD_APPROVED, FRAUD_DECLINE_CODE, WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
    WS_FRAUD_RESPONSE = WsFraudResponseC12()
    if FRAUD_SCORE < 70:
        WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'N'
        WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE  = None  # TODO: was FRAUD_DECLINE_CODE

def check_available_credit_C12() -> None:
    """Checks available credit."""
    global WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC, WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT, WS_AUTH_AMOUNT, WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE, WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
    WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC = WsCardAccountRecC12()
    if WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT >= WS_AUTH_AMOUNT:
        WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'N'
        WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = '51'

def approve_auth_C12() -> None:
    """Approves authorization."""
    global WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE, WS_AUTH_AMOUNT, WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT
    WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = '00'
    generate_auth_code_C12()
    WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT -= None  # TODO: was WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
    record_authorization_C12()

def generate_auth_code_C12() -> None:
    """Generates authorization code."""
    global WS_AUTH_CODE, WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE
    WS_AUTH_CODE = 0 #int(random.random() * 999999)
    WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE = WsAuthResponseAuthCodeC12()

def record_authorization_C12() -> None:
    """Records authorization details."""
    global WS_AUTH_RECORD, WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, AUTH_REC_CARD, WS_AUTH_AMOUNT, AUTH_REC_AMOUNT, WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE, AUTH_REC_CODE, WS_PROCESS_DATE, AUTH_REC_DATE, AUTH_REC_TIME, WS_MERCHANT_ID, AUTH_REC_MERCHANT, AUTH_REC_STATUS, AUTH_RECORD
    WS_AUTH_RECORD = WsAuthRecordC12()
    AUTH_REC_CARD  = None  # TODO: was WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
    AUTH_REC_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
    AUTH_REC_CODE  = None  # TODO: was WS_AUTH_CODE
    AUTH_REC_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    AUTH_REC_TIME = "CURRENT_TIME"
    AUTH_REC_MERCHANT  = None  # TODO: was WS_MERCHANT_ID
    AUTH_REC_STATUS = 'P'
    AUTH_RECORD = AuthRecordC12()

def decline_auth_C12() -> None:
    """Declines authorization."""
    global WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE, WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE, WS_DECLINE_RECORD, WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, DECLINE_REC_CARD, WS_AUTH_AMOUNT, DECLINE_REC_AMOUNT, DECLINE_REC_CODE, WS_PROCESS_DATE, DECLINE_REC_DATE, DECLINE_RECORD
    WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
    WS_DECLINE_RECORD = WsDeclineRecordC12()
    DECLINE_REC_CARD  = None  # TODO: was WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
    DECLINE_REC_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
    DECLINE_REC_CODE = WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
    DECLINE_REC_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    DECLINE_RECORD = DeclineRecordC12()

def capture_transaction_C12() -> None:
    """Captures a transaction."""
    global WS_CAPTURE_REQUEST, WS_AUTH_VALID
    if WS_CAPTURE_REQUEST == 'Y':
        validate_auth_code_C12()
        if WS_AUTH_VALID == 'Y':
            create_capture_record_C12()

def validate_auth_code_C12() -> None:
    """Validates authorization code."""
    global WS_AUTH_VALID, WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE, AUTH_SEARCH_KEY, WS_AUTH_REC, AUTH_REC_STATUS, AUTH_FILE
    WS_AUTH_VALID = 'N'
    AUTH_SEARCH_KEY = WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE
    WS_AUTH_REC = WsAuthRecC12()
    if AUTH_REC_STATUS == 'P':
        WS_AUTH_VALID = 'Y'

def create_capture_record_C12() -> None:
    """Creates capture record."""
    global AUTH_REC_STATUS, WS_AUTH_REC, WS_CAPTURE_RECORD, AUTH_REC_CARD, CAPTURE_CARD, WS_CAPTURE_AMOUNT, CAPTURE_AMOUNT, WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE, CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE, WS_PROCESS_DATE, CAPTURE_DATE, CAPTURE_RECORD
    AUTH_REC_STATUS = 'C'
    WS_CAPTURE_RECORD = WsCaptureRecordC12()
    CAPTURE_CARD  = None  # TODO: was AUTH_REC_CARD
    CAPTURE_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_CAPTURE_AMOUNT
    CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE = WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE
    CAPTURE_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    CAPTURE_RECORD = CaptureRecordC12()

def process_settlement_C12() -> None:
    """Processes settlement."""
    batch_transactions_C12()
    calculate_fees_C12()
    create_funding_record_C12()
    send_settlement_file_C12()

def batch_transactions_C12() -> None:
    """Batches transactions for settlement."""
    global WS_BATCH_TOTAL, WS_BATCH_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_CAPTURE_REC, CAPTURE_SETTLED
    WS_BATCH_TOTAL = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_BATCH_COUNT = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
        WS_CAPTURE_REC = WsCaptureRecordC12()
        if WS_EOF_FLAG == 'Y':
            pass
        else:
            if CAPTURE_SETTLED == 'N':
                #ADD capture_amount TO ws_batch_total
                WS_BATCH_TOTAL += 0
                WS_BATCH_COUNT += 1
                CAPTURE_SETTLED = 'Y'
                #REWRITE capture_record FROM ws_capture_rec
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def calculate_fees_C12() -> None:
    """Calculates fees for settlement."""
    global WS_BATCH_TOTAL, WS_INTERCHANGE_FEE, WS_ASSESSMENT_FEE, WS_PROCESSOR_FEE, WS_BATCH_COUNT, WS_TOTAL_FEES
    WS_INTERCHANGE_FEE = WS_BATCH_TOTAL * Decimal("0.0175")
    WS_ASSESSMENT_FEE = WS_BATCH_TOTAL * Decimal("0.0015")
    WS_PROCESSOR_FEE = Decimal(WS_BATCH_COUNT) * Decimal("0.10")
    WS_TOTAL_FEES  = None  # TODO: was WS_INTERCHANGE_F

    global ws_formatted_error_C13
    ws_formatted_error_C13 = f"ERROR: {ws_error_code_C13} - {ws_error_msg_C13}"

def display_error_C13() -> None:
    """Displays the formatted error message."""
    print(ws_formatted_error_C13)

def write_error_log_C13() -> None:
    """Writes the error log."""
    global ws_error_log_rec_C13 # Assuming ws_error_log_rec_C13 is defined globally
    ws_error_log_rec_C13 = {} # Initialize as dictionary

    ws_error_log_rec_C13['err_log_code'] = ws_error_code_C13 # Assuming ws_error_code_C13 is defined globally
    ws_error_log_rec_C13['err_log_msg'] = ws_error_msg_C13  # Assuming ws_error_msg_C13 is defined globally
    ws_error_log_rec_C13['err_log_timestamp'] = datetime.now()
    ws_error_log_rec_C13['err_log_program'] = ws_program_name_C13 # Assuming ws_program_name_C13 is defined globally
    ws_error_log_rec_C13['err_log_paragraph'] = ws_paragraph_name_C13 # Assuming ws_paragraph_name_C13 is defined globally

    # Assuming write_error_log_record is defined and takes appropriate arguments
    write_error_log_record_C13(ws_error_log_rec_C13)

def treasury_management_C13() -> None:
    """Manages treasury functions."""
    calculate_cash_position_C13()
    project_cash_flows_C13()
    manage_reserves_C13()
    manage_investments_C13()
    manage_borrowings_C13()

def calculate_cash_position_C13() -> None:
    """Calculates the cash position."""
    global ws_treasury_management_C13
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_cash_position = Decimal("0.00")
    sum_vault_cash_C13()
    sum_fed_account_C13()
    sum_correspondent_balances_C13()

def sum_vault_cash_C13() -> None:
    """Sums the vault cash."""
    global ws_eof_flag_C13, ws_treasury_management_C13
    ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C13 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_vault_rec_C13 = read_vault_cash_file_C13() # Assuming read_vault_cash_file_C13 is defined
            ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_cash_position += ws_vault_rec_C13.vault_balance  # Assuming vault_balance attribute
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'N'

def sum_fed_account_C13() -> None:
    """Sums the fed account."""
    global ws_treasury_management_C13
    ws_fed_balance_C13 = read_fed_account_file_C13() # Assuming read_fed_account_file_C13 is defined
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_cash_position += ws_fed_balance_C13 # Assuming ws_fed_balance_C13 is Decimal

def sum_correspondent_balances_C13() -> None:
    """Sums the correspondent balances."""
    global ws_eof_flag_C13, ws_treasury_management_C13
    ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C13 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_corr_rec_C13 = read_correspondent_file_C13() # Assuming read_correspondent_file_C13 is defined
            ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_cash_position += ws_corr_rec_C13.corr_balance # Assuming corr_balance attribute
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'N'

def project_cash_flows_C13() -> None:
    """Projects the cash flows."""
    global ws_treasury_management_C13
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_projected_inflows = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_projected_outflows = Decimal("0.00")
    project_loan_payments_C13()
    project_deposit_flows_C13()
    project_investment_maturities_C13()
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_net_position = ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_cash_position + ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_projected_inflows - ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_projected_outflows

def project_loan_payments_C13() -> None:
    """Projects the loan payments."""
    global ws_eof_flag_C13, ws_treasury_management_C13, ws_projection_date_C13
    ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C13 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_loan_pmt_rec_C13 = read_loan_schedule_file_C13() # Assuming read_loan_schedule_file_C13 is defined
            if ws_loan_pmt_rec_C13.loan_pmt_date <= ws_projection_date_C13:
                ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_projected_inflows += ws_loan_pmt_rec_C13.loan_pmt_amount  # Assuming loan_pmt_amount attribute
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'N'

def project_deposit_flows_C13() -> None:
    """Projects the deposit flows."""
    global ws_treasury_management_C13, ws_avg_daily_deposits_C13, ws_avg_daily_withdrawals_C13, ws_projection_days_C13
    ws_expected_deposits_C13 = ws_avg_daily_deposits_C13 * ws_projection_days_C13
    ws_expected_withdrawals_C13 = ws_avg_daily_withdrawals_C13 * ws_projection_days_C13
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_projected_inflows += ws_expected_deposits_C13
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_projected_outflows += ws_expected_withdrawals_C13

def project_investment_maturities_C13() -> None:
    """Projects the investment maturities."""
    global ws_eof_flag_C13, ws_treasury_management_C13, ws_projection_date_C13
    ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C13 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_inv_rec_C13 = read_investment_file_C13() # Assuming read_investment_file_C13 is defined
            if ws_inv_rec_C13.inv_maturity_date <= ws_projection_date_C13:
                ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_projected_inflows += ws_inv_rec_C13.inv_par_value  # Assuming inv_par_value attribute
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'N'

def manage_reserves_C13() -> None:
    """Manages the reserves."""
    calculate_reserve_requirement_C13()
    check_reserve_position_C13()
    if ws_reserve_deficiency_C13 == 'Y': # Assuming ws_reserve_deficiency_C13 is defined
        cover_reserve_shortfall_C13()
    else:
        invest_excess_reserves_C13()

def calculate_reserve_requirement_C13() -> None:
    """Calculates the reserve requirement."""
    global ws_treasury_management_C13, ws_total_deposits_C13, ws_reserve_ratio_C13
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_reserve_requirement = ws_total_deposits_C13 * ws_reserve_ratio_C13

def check_reserve_position_C13() -> None:
    """Checks the reserve position."""
    global ws_treasury_management_C13, ws_fed_balance_C13, ws_reserve_deficiency_C13
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_excess_reserves = ws_fed_balance_C13 - ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_reserve_requirement
    if ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_excess_reserves < 0:
        ws_reserve_deficiency_C13 = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_reserve_deficiency_C13 = 'N'

def cover_reserve_shortfall_C13() -> None:
    """Covers the reserve shortfall."""
    global ws_treasury_management_C13
    ws_shortfall_amount_C13 = Decimal("0.00") - ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_excess_reserves
    borrow_fed_funds_C13()

def borrow_fed_funds_C13() -> None:
    """Borrows fed funds."""
    global ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13, ws_shortfall_amount_C13, ws_fed_funds_rate_C13, ws_process_date_C13
    ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13 = {}  # Assuming ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13 is a dictionary
    ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13['ff_trans_type'] = 'BORROW'
    ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13['ff_amount'] = ws_shortfall_amount_C13
    ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13['ff_rate'] = ws_fed_funds_rate_C13
    ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13['ff_settle_date'] = ws_process_date_C13
    ff_maturity_date_C13 = int(ws_process_date_C13.strftime("%Y%m%d")) + 1
    ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13['ff_maturity_date'] = ff_maturity_date_C13
    write_fed_funds_record_C13(ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13) # Assuming write_fed_funds_record_C13 is defined

def invest_excess_reserves_C13() -> None:
    """Invests the excess reserves."""
    global ws_treasury_management_C13, ws_min_invest_amount_C13
    if ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_excess_reserves > ws_min_invest_amount_C13:
        sell_fed_funds_C13()

def sell_fed_funds_C13() -> None:
    """Sells fed funds."""
    global ws_treasury_management_C13, ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13, ws_fed_funds_rate_C13, ws_process_date_C13
    ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13 = {}  # Assuming ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13 is a dictionary
    ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13['ff_trans_type'] = 'SELL'
    ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13['ff_amount'] = ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_excess_reserves
    ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13['ff_rate'] = ws_fed_funds_rate_C13
    ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13['ff_settle_date'] = ws_process_date_C13
    ff_maturity_date_C13 = int(ws_process_date_C13.strftime("%Y%m%d")) + 1
    ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13['ff_maturity_date'] = ff_maturity_date_C13
    write_fed_funds_record_C13(ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13) # Assuming write_fed_funds_record_C13 is defined

def manage_investments_C13() -> None:
    """Manages the investments."""
    review_investment_portfolio_C13()
    execute_investment_strategy_C13()
    mark_to_market_C13()

def review_investment_portfolio_C13() -> None:
    """Reviews the investment portfolio."""
    global ws_eof_flag_C13, ws_treasury_management_C13, ws_total_yield_C13, ws_total_duration_C13, ws_inv_count_C13, ws_avg_yield_C13, ws_avg_duration_C13
    ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_investment_pool = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_avg_yield_C13 = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_avg_duration_C13 = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_total_yield_C13 = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_total_duration_C13 = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_inv_count_C13 = 0
    ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C13 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_inv_rec_C13 = read_investment_file_C13() # Assuming read_investment_file_C13 is defined
            ws_treasury_management_C13.ws_investment_pool += ws_inv_rec_C13.inv_market_value  # Assuming inv_market_value attribute
            ws_total_yield_C13 += ws_inv_rec_C13.inv_yield   # Assuming inv_yield attribute
            ws_total_duration_C13 += ws_inv_rec_C13.inv_duration # Assuming inv_duration attribute
            ws_inv_count_C13 += 1
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'Y'
    if ws_inv_count_C13 > 0:
        ws_avg_yield_C13 = ws_total_yield_C13 / ws_inv_count_C13
        ws_avg_duration_C13 = ws_total_duration_C13 / ws_inv_count_C13
    ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'N'

def execute_investment_strategy_C13() -> None:
    """Executes the investment strategy."""
    if ws_rate_outlook_C13 == 'RISING': # Assuming ws_rate_outlook_C13 is defined
        shorten_duration_C13()
    elif ws_rate_outlook_C13 == 'FALLING':
        extend_duration_C13()
    elif ws_rate_outlook_C13 == 'STABLE':
        maintain_position_C13()

def shorten_duration_C13() -> None:
    """Shortens the duration."""
    print('STRATEGY: SHORTENING PORTFOLIO DURATION')

def extend_duration_C13() -> None:
    """Extends the duration."""
    print('STRATEGY: EXTENDING PORTFOLIO DURATION')

def maintain_position_C13() -> None:
    """Maintains the position."""
    print('STRATEGY: MAINTAINING CURRENT POSITION')

def mark_to_market_C13() -> None:
    """Marks to market."""
    global ws_eof_flag_C13
    ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C13 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_inv_rec_C13 = read_investment_file_C13() # Assuming read_investment_file_C13 is defined
            get_market_price_C13(ws_inv_rec_C13) #Passing the record to the function
            ws_inv_rec_C13.inv_market_value = ws_inv_rec_C13.inv_par_value * ws_market_price_C13 / 100   # Assuming inv_par_value and inv_market_value attributes
            ws_inv_rec_C13.inv_unrealized_gl = ws_inv_rec_C13.inv_market_value - ws_inv_rec_C13.inv_book_value # Assuming inv_book_value attribute
            rewrite_investment_record_C13(ws_inv_rec_C13) # Assuming rewrite_investment_record_C13 is defined
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'N'

def get_market_price_C13(ws_inv_rec_C13) -> None:
    """Gets the market price."""

    ws_cusip_lookup_C13 = ws_inv_rec_C13.inv_cusip # Assuming inv_cusip attribute
    ws_market_price_C13 = bondprice_C13(ws_cusip_lookup_C13)  # Assuming bondprice_C13 is defined and returns a Decimal

def optimize_funding_mix_C13() -> None:
    """Optimizes the funding mix."""
    global ws_total_int_expense_C13, ws_total_deposits_C13, ws_deposit_cost_C13, ws_wholesale_rate_C13
    ws_deposit_cost_C13 = (ws_total_int_expense_C13 / ws_total_deposits_C13) * 100
    if ws_deposit_cost_C13 > ws_wholesale_rate_C13:
        print('CONSIDER WHOLESALE FUNDING')

def manage_maturities_C13() -> None:
    """Manages the maturities."""
    global ws_eof_flag_C13, ws_process_date_C13
    ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C13 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_borrow_rec_C13 = read_borrowing_file_C13() # Assuming read_borrowing_file_C13 is defined
            borrow_maturity_date = datetime.strptime(str(ws_borrow_rec_C13.borrow_maturity), '%Y%m%d').date() # Assuming borrow_maturity attribute and converting from int to date
            process_date_plus_7 = ws_process_date_C13.date() + timedelta(days=7)  # Add 7 days to process date, extracting date
            if borrow_maturity_date <= process_date_plus_7: #Comparison requires date objects
                rollover_decision_C13(ws_borrow_rec_C13) #Passes ws_borrow_rec_C13 as argument
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C13 = 'N'


def rollover_decision_C13(ws_borrow_rec_C13) -> None:
    """Makes rollover decision."""
    global ws_cash_position_C13

    if ws_cash_position_C13 >= ws_borrow_rec_C13.borrow_amount:  # Assuming borrow_amount is attribute
        repay_borrowing_C13(ws_borrow_rec_C13) #Pass ws_borrow_rec_C13 as argument
    else:
        rollover_borrowing_C13(ws_borrow_rec_C13) #Pass ws_borrow_rec_C13 as argument

def check_reconciliation_difference_C15(ws_gl_control_bal: Decimal, ws_subledger_total: Decimal) -> None:
    """Check reconciliation difference and log if necessary."""
    ws_recon_diff = ws_gl_control_bal - ws_subledger_total
    if ws_recon_diff != Decimal('0'):
        log_reconciliation_exception_C15()

def log_reconciliation_exception_C15() -> None:
    """Log a reconciliation exception."""
    initialize_ws_recon_exception_C15()
    recon_exc_account = ws_gl_account_C15
    recon_exc_diff = ws_recon_diff_C15
    recon_exc_date = current_date_C15()
    write_recon_exception_record_C15(ws_recon_exception_C15)

def intercompany_reconciliation_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    load_intercompany_balances_C15()
    match_intercompany_pairs_C15()
    report_intercompany_differences_C15()

def load_intercompany_balances_C15() -> None:
    """Load intercompany balances from file."""
    global ws_ic_count_C15, ws_eof_flag_C15
    ws_ic_count_C15 = 0
    ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C15 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_ic_balance_C15 = read_intercompany_file_C15()
            ws_ic_count_C15 += 1
            ws_ic_array_C15[ws_ic_count_C15] = ws_ic_balance_C15
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'

def match_intercompany_pairs_C15() -> None:
    """Match intercompany balance pairs."""
    ws_ic_idx_C15 = 1
    while ws_ic_idx_C15 <= ws_ic_count_C15:
        find_intercompany_counterpart_C15(ws_ic_idx_C15)
        ws_ic_idx_C15 += 1

def find_intercompany_counterpart_C15(ws_ic_idx: int) -> None:
    """Find counterpart for an intercompany balance."""
    global ws_search_from_C15, ws_search_to_C15
    ws_search_from_C15 = ic_from_entity_C15[ws_ic_idx - 1]
    ws_search_to_C15 = ic_to_entity_C15[ws_ic_idx - 1]
    ws_ic_idx2 = 1
    while ws_ic_idx2 <= ws_ic_count_C15:
        if ic_from_entity_C15[ws_ic_idx2 - 1] == ws_search_to_C15:
            if ic_to_entity_C15[ws_ic_idx2 - 1] == ws_search_from_C15:
                ws_ic_diff_C15 = ic_amount_C15[ws_ic_idx - 1] + ic_amount_C15[ws_ic_idx2 - 1]
                if ws_ic_diff_C15 != Decimal('0'):
                    log_intercompany_difference_C15()
                break
        ws_ic_idx2 += 1

def log_intercompany_difference_C15() -> None:
    """Log an intercompany difference."""
    initialize_ws_ic_diff_rec_C15()
    icd_from = ws_search_from_C15
    icd_to = ws_search_to_C15
    icd_amount = ws_ic_diff_C15
    write_ic_diff_record_C15(ws_ic_diff_rec_C15)

def report_intercompany_differences_C15() -> None:
    """Report intercompany differences."""
    print('INTERCOMPANY RECONCILIATION COMPLETE')

def move_to_archive_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ws_eof_flag_C15
    ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C15 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_audit_record_C15 = read_audit_file_C15()
            if ws_audit_timestamp_C15 < ws_archive_date_C15:
                write_archive_audit_record_C15(ws_audit_record_C15)
                delete_audit_file_C15()
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'

def compress_archive_C15() -> None:
    """Compress the audit archive."""
    print('COMPRESSING AUDIT ARCHIVE')

def transaction_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """Collect transaction metrics."""
    global ws_tps_C15, ws_avg_response_C15
    ws_tps_C15 = ws_trans_count_C15 / ws_elapsed_seconds_C15
    ws_avg_response_C15 = ws_total_response_time_C15 / ws_trans_count_C15

def analyze_performance_C15() -> None:
    """Analyze performance metrics."""
    global ws_perf_degraded_C15, ws_throughput_low_C15
    if ws_avg_response_C15 > ws_response_threshold_C15:
        ws_perf_degraded_C15 = 'Y'
    if ws_tps_C15 < ws_min_tps_threshold_C15:
        ws_throughput_low_C15 = 'Y'

        if ws_backup_status_C15 == 'SUCCESS':

def encrypt_ssn_C15() -> None:
    """Encrypt Social Security Number."""
    ws_encrypt_input_C15 = ws_plain_ssn_C15
    get_aes256enc_C15(ws_encrypt_input_C15, ws_encryption_key_C15, ws_encrypted_ssn_C15)
    cust_ssn_encrypted = ws_encrypted_ssn_C15

def encrypt_account_number_C15() -> None:
    """Encrypt Account Number."""
    ws_encrypt_input_C15 = ws_plain_account_C15
    get_aes256enc_C15(ws_encrypt_input_C15, ws_encryption_key_C15, ws_encrypted_account_C15)
    acct_number_encrypted = ws_encrypted_account_C15

    get_hashpin_C15(ws_encrypt_input_C15, ws_hashed_pin_C15)

def key_management_C15() -> None:
    """Manage encryption keys."""
    rotate_encryption_key_C15()
    backup_keys_C15()
    audit_key_usage_C15()

def rotate_encryption_key_C15() -> None:
    """Rotate the encryption key."""
    if ws_key_age_days_C15 > 90:
        get_genkey_C15(ws_new_key_C15)
        ws_old_key_C15 = ws_encryption_key_C15
        ws_encryption_key_C15 = ws_new_key_C15
        reencrypt_data_C15()

def reencrypt_data_C15() -> None:
    """Re-encrypt data with the new encryption key."""
    global ws_eof_flag_C15
    ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C15 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_enc_record_C15 = read_encrypted_data_file_C15()
            get_aes256dec_C15(enc_data_C15, ws_old_key_C15, ws_decrypted_data_C15)
            get_aes256enc_C15(ws_decrypted_data_C15, ws_encryption_key_C15, ws_reencrypted_data_C15)
            enc_data_C15 = ws_reencrypted_data_C15
            rewrite_encrypted_data_record_C15(ws_enc_record_C15)
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'

def backup_keys_C15() -> None:
    """Backup encryption keys."""
    get_keybackup_C15(ws_encryption_key_C15, ws_backup_status_C15)
    if ws_backup_status_C15 == 'SUCCESS':
        ws_last_key_backup_C15 = current_date_C15()

def audit_key_usage_C15() -> None:
    """Audit encryption key usage."""
    initialize_ws_key_audit_rec_C15()
    key_audit_id = ws_key_id_C15
    key_audit_operation = ws_key_operation_C15
    key_audit_timestamp = current_date_C15()
    key_audit_user = ws_user_id_C15
    write_key_audit_record_C15(ws_key_audit_rec_C15)

def access_control_C15() -> None:
    """Control access to resources."""
    authenticate_user_C15()
    authorize_action_C15()
    log_access_C15()

def authenticate_user_C15() -> None:
    """Authenticate a user."""
    global ws_auth_success_C15, ws_failed_auth_count_C15
    ws_auth_success_C15 = 'N'
    get_authuser_C15(ws_username_C15, ws_password_C15, ws_auth_result_C15)
    if ws_auth_result_C15 == 'SUCCESS':
        ws_auth_success_C15 = 'Y'
        create_session_C15()
    else:
        log_failed_auth_C15()

def create_session_C15() -> None:
    """Create a user session."""
    global ws_session_id_C15, ws_session_start_C15, ws_session_expiry_C15
    ws_session_id_C15 = random_number_C15() * 999999999999
    ws_session_start_C15 = current_date_C15()
    ws_session_expiry_C15 = integer_of_date_C15(ws_session_start_C15) + 1

def log_failed_auth_C15() -> None:
    """Log a failed authentication attempt."""
    global ws_failed_auth_count_C15
    ws_failed_auth_count_C15 += 1
    if ws_failed_auth_count_C15 >= 3:
        lock_account_C15()

def lock_account_C15() -> None:
    """Lock a user account."""
    user_status = 'L'
    user_lock_date = current_date_C15()
    rewrite_user_record_C15(ws_user_rec_C15)

def authorize_action_C15() -> None:
    """Authorize a user action."""
    global ws_authorized_C15
    ws_authorized_C15 = 'N'
    role_search_key = ws_user_role_C15
    ws_role_perm_C15 = read_role_permission_file_C15(role_search_key)
    if ws_requested_action_C15 == role_permitted_action_C15:
        ws_authorized_C15 = 'Y'

    access_log_result = ws_authorized_C15

    """Detect security anomalies."""

    if ws_login_count_C15 > ws_normal_login_threshold_C15:
        ws_anomaly_detected_C15 = 'Y'
        ws_anomaly_type_C15 = 'EXCESSIVE LOGINS'
    if ws_trans_volume_C15 > ws_normal_trans_threshold_C15:
        ws_anomaly_detected_C15 = 'Y'
        ws_anomaly_type_C15 = 'HIGH TRANSACTION VOLUME'

        alert_security_team_C15()

def crm_procedures_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    customer_segmentation_C15()
    cross_sell_analysis_C15()
    retention_analysis_C15()
    customer_profitability_C15()

def customer_segmentation_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ws_eof_flag_C15
    ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C15 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_cust_rec_C15 = read_customer_file_C15()
            calculate_segment_C15()
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'

def calculate_segment_C15() -> None:
    """Calculate customer segment."""
    global cust_segment_C15
    ws_relationship_value = (cust_total_deposits_C15 + cust_loan_balances_C15 + cust_investment_value_C15)
    if ws_relationship_value >= 1000000:
        cust_segment_C15 = 'private_bank'
    elif ws_relationship_value >= 250000:
        cust_segment_C15 = 'wealth_mgmt'
    elif ws_relationship_value >= 100000:
        cust_segment_C15 = 'PREFERRED'
    elif ws_relationship_value >= 25000:
        cust_segment_C15 = 'CORE'
    else:
        cust_segment_C15 = 'BASIC'
    rewrite_customer_record_C15(ws_cust_rec_C15)

def cross_sell_analysis_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ws_eof_flag_C15
    ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C15 != 'Y':
     def identify_opportunities_C15() -> None:
    """Identify cross-sell opportunities."""
    if cust_has_checking_C15 == 'Y' and cust_has_savings_C15 == 'N':
        ws_opportunity_C15 = 'SAVINGS'
        create_lead_C15()
    if cust_has_mortgage_C15 == 'N' and cust_income_C15 > 75000:
        ws_opportunity_C15 = 'MORTGAGE'
        create_lead_C15()
    if cust_has_investment_C15 == 'N' and cust_total_deposits_C15 > 50000:
        ws_opportunity_C15 = 'INVESTMENT'
        create_lead_C15()

def create_lead_C15() -> None:
    """Create a cross-sell lead."""
    initialize_ws_lead_record_C15()
    lead_customer = cust_id_C15
    lead_product = ws_opportunity_C15
    lead_create_date = current_date_C15()
    lead_status = 'NEW'
    write_lead_record_C15(ws_lead_record_C15)

def retention_analysis_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ws_eof_flag_C15
    ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C15 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_cust_rec_C15 = read_customer_file_C15()
            calculate_churn_risk_C15()
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'

def calculate_churn_risk_C15() -> None:
    """Calculate customer churn risk."""
    global cust_churn_risk_C15
    ws_churn_score = 0
    if cust_balance_trend_C15 == 'DECLINING':
        ws_churn_score += 25
    if cust_trans_frequency_C15 == 'LOW':
        ws_churn_score += 20
    if cust_complaint_count_C15 > 2:
        ws_churn_score += 30
    if cust_tenure_months_C15 < 12:
        ws_churn_score += 15
    cust_churn_risk_C15 = ws_churn_score
    if ws_churn_score > 50:
        create_retention_alert_C15()
    rewrite_customer_record_C15(ws_cust_rec_C15)

def create_retention_alert_C15() -> None:
    """Create a retention alert."""
    initialize_ws_retention_alert_C15()
    retain_customer = cust_id_C15
    retain_risk_score = ws_churn_score
    retain_alert_date = current_date_C15()
    write_retention_alert_record_C15(ws_retention_alert_C15)

def customer_profitability_C15() -> None:
    """Calculate customer profitability."""
    global ws_eof_flag_C15
    ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C15 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_cust_rec_C15 = read_customer_file_C15()
            calculate_profitability_C15()
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'

def calculate_profitability_C15() -> None:
    """Calculate customer profitability."""
    global cust_profitability_C15
    ws_interest_margin = (cust_loan_interest_C15 - cust_deposit_interest_C15)
    ws_fee_income = cust_service_fees_C15 + cust_trans_fees_C15
    ws_cost_to_serve = (cust_branch_visits_C15 * 5 + cust_call_count_C15 * 3 + cust_online_trans_C15 * 0.10)
    cust_profitability_C15 = ws_interest_margin + ws_fee_income - ws_cost_to_serve
